```
1→"""Signature variance detection — same function name, different signatures across modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import FunctionInfo
     8→
     9→# Names that are legitimately polymorphic — skip them
    10→_ALLOWLIST = {
    11→    "__init__",
    12→    "__repr__",
    13→    "__str__",
    14→    "__eq__",
    15→    "__hash__",
    16→    "__lt__",
    17→    "__le__",
    18→    "__gt__",
    19→    "__ge__",
    20→    "__len__",
    21→    "__iter__",
    22→    "__next__",
    23→    "__enter__",
    24→    "__exit__",
    25→    "__call__",
    26→    "__getattr__",
    27→    "__setattr__",
    28→    "__getitem__",
    29→    "__setitem__",
    30→    "__delitem__",
    31→    "__contains__",
    32→    "main",
    33→    "setup",
    34→    "teardown",
    35→    "configure",
    36→    "run",
    37→    "handle",
    38→    "setUp",
    39→    "tearDown",
    40→    "setUpClass",
    41→    "tearDownClass",
    42→    "get",
    43→    "post",
    44→    "put",
    45→    "delete",
    46→    "patch",  # HTTP methods
    47→}
    48→
    49→
    50→def _signature_group_keys(name: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    51→    """Return grouping keys: exact name and selected naming-pattern buckets."""
    52→    keys = [("name", name)]
    53→    normalized = name.lstrip("_")
    54→    if normalized.startswith("phase_") and len(normalized) > len("phase_"):
    55→        keys.append(("pattern", "phase_*"))
    56→    return keys
    57→
    58→
    59→def detect_signature_variance(
    60→    functions: list[FunctionInfo],
    61→    min_occurrences: int = 3,
    62→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    63→    """Find function names appearing 3+ times across files with different signatures.
    64→
    65→    Returns (entries, total_functions_checked).
    66→
    67→    Each entry represents a function name group where signatures differ:
    68→    {
    69→        "name": str,           # function name
    70→        "occurrences": int,    # how many definitions
    71→        "files": list[str],    # distinct files
    72→        "variants": list[dict] # [{file, line, params, param_count}]
    73→    }
    74→    """
    75→    # Group by exact function name and select naming-pattern groups.
    76→    by_group: dict[tuple[str, str], list[FunctionInfo]] = defaultdict(list)
    77→    for fn in functions:
    78→        normalized_name = fn.name.lstrip("_")
    79→        is_phase_pattern = normalized_name.startswith("phase_")
    80→        if fn.name.startswith("_") and not fn.name.startswith("__") and not is_phase_pattern:
    81→            continue  # Skip private functions — expected to be independent
    82→        if fn.name in _ALLOWLIST:
    83→            continue
    84→        if fn.name.startswith("test_"):
    85→            continue  # Skip test functions
    86→        for key in _signature_group_keys(fn.name):
    87→            by_group[key].append(fn)
    88→
    89→    entries = []
    90→    for (group_type, group_name), fns in by_group.items():
    91→        # Need at least min_occurrences across different files
    92→        distinct_files = set(getattr(f, "file", "") for f in fns)
    93→        if len(distinct_files) < min_occurrences:
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        # Compare parameter and return signatures (ignore self/cls params).
    97→        variants = []
    98→        for fn in fns:
    99→            params = [p for p in fn.params if p not in ("self", "cls")]
   100→            variants.append(
   101→                {
   102→                    "file": getattr(fn, "file", ""),
   103→                    "line": fn.line,
   104→                    "params": params,
   105→                    "param_count": len(params),
   106→                    "return_annotation": getattr(fn, "return_annotation", None),
   107→                }
   108→            )
   109→
   110→        param_signatures = set()
   111→        for v in variants:
   112→            param_signatures.add(tuple(v["params"]))
   113→        return_signatures = {
   114→            str(v["return_annotation"]).strip()
   115→            for v in variants
   116→            if v.get("return_annotation")
   117→        }
   118→        has_param_variance = len(param_signatures) >= 2
   119→        has_return_variance = len(return_signatures) >= 2
   120→
   121→        # All identical or not enough annotated return variants.
   122→        if not has_param_variance and not has_return_variance:
   123→            continue
   124→
   125→        entries.append(
   126→            {
   127→                "name": group_name,
   128→                "group_type": group_type,
   129→                "occurrences": len(fns),
   130→                "files": sorted(distinct_files),
   131→                "file_count": len(distinct_files),
   132→                "variants": variants,
   133→                "signature_count": len(param_signatures),
   134→                "return_signature_count": len(return_signatures),
   135→                "has_param_variance": has_param_variance,
   136→                "has_return_variance": has_return_variance,
   137→            }
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    entries.sort(
   141→        key=lambda e: (
   142→            -max(e["signature_count"], e["return_signature_count"]),
   143→            -e["occurrences"],
   144→            e["name"],
   145→        )
   146→    )
   147→    return entries, len(functions)
   148→
```

```
    56→def phase_dupes(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    57→    """Shared phase runner: detect duplicate functions via lang.extract_functions.
    58→
    59→    When a zone map is available, filters out functions from zone-excluded files
    60→    before the O(n^2) comparison to avoid test/config/generated false positives.
    61→    """
    62→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path)
    63→
    64→    # Filter out functions from zone-excluded files.
    65→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
    66→        before = len(functions)
    67→        functions = [
    68→            f
    69→            for f in functions
    70→            if lang.zone_map.get(getattr(f, "file", "")) not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
    71→        ]
    72→        excluded = before - len(functions)
    73→        if excluded:
    74→            log(f"         zones: {excluded} functions excluded (non-production)")
    75→
    76→    entries, total_functions = detect_duplicates(functions)
    77→    findings = make_dupe_findings(entries, log)
    78→    return findings, {"dupes": total_functions}
    79→
    80→
    81→def phase_boilerplate_duplication(
    82→    path: Path,
    83→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
    84→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    85→    """Shared phase runner: detect repeated boilerplate code via jscpd."""
    86→    entries = detect_with_jscpd(path)
    87→    if entries is None:
    88→        return [], {}
    89→    entries = _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone(entries, lang.zone_map)
    90→
    91→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    92→    for entry in entries:
    93→        locations = entry["locations"]
    94→        first = locations[0]
    95→        loc_preview = ", ".join(
    96→            f"{rel(item['file'])}:{item['line']}" for item in locations[:4]
    97→        )
    98→        if len(locations) > 4:
    99→            loc_preview += f", +{len(locations) - 4} more"
   100→        findings.append(
   101→            make_finding(
   102→                "boilerplate_duplication",
   103→                first["file"],
   104→                entry["id"],
   105→                tier=3,
   106→                confidence="medium",
   107→                summary=(
   108→                    f"Boilerplate block repeated across {entry['distinct_files']} files "
   109→                    f"(window {entry['window_size']} lines): {loc_preview}"
   110→                ),
   111→                detail={
   112→                    "distinct_files": entry["distinct_files"],
   113→                    "window_size": entry["window_size"],
   114→                    "locations": locations,
   115→                    "sample": entry["sample"],
   116→                },
   117→            )
   118→        )
   119→
   120→    if findings:
   121→        log(f"         boilerplate duplication: {len(findings)} clusters")
   122→    distinct_files = len({loc["file"] for e in entries for loc in e["locations"]})
   123→    return findings, {"boilerplate_duplication": distinct_files}
   124→
   125→
   126→def _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone(entries: list[dict], zone_map) -> list[dict]:
   127→    """Keep only in-scope, zone-allowed boilerplate clusters.
   128→
   129→    jscpd can return files that are outside language discovery (artifacts/docs).
   130→    Restricting to known zone-map files prevents unknown paths from being treated
   131→    as production findings.
   132→    """
   133→    if zone_map is None:
   134→        return entries
   135→
   136→    known_files = set(zone_map.all_files())
   137→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   138→    skipped = 0
   139→    for entry in entries:
   140→        locations = entry.get("locations", [])
   141→        kept_locations = [
   142→            loc
   143→            for loc in locations
   144→            if loc.get("file") in known_files
   145→            and not should_skip_finding(
   146→                zone_map,
   147→                loc.get("file", ""),
   148→                "boilerplate_duplication",
   149→            )
   150→        ]
   151→        distinct_files = {loc["file"] for loc in kept_locations}
   152→        if len(distinct_files) < 2:
   153→            skipped += 1
   154→            continue
   155→
   156→        normalized = dict(entry)
   157→        normalized["locations"] = sorted(
   158→            kept_locations,
   159→            key=lambda item: (item.get("file", ""), item.get("line", 0)),
   160→        )
   161→        normalized["distinct_files"] = len(distinct_files)
   162→        filtered.append(normalized)
   163→
   164→    if skipped:
   165→        log(f"         zones: {skipped} boilerplate clusters excluded")
   166→    return filtered
   167→
   168→
   169→def find_external_test_files(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> set[str]:
   170→    """Find test files in standard locations outside the scanned path."""
   171→    extra = set()
   172→    path_root = path.resolve()
   173→    project_root = get_project_root()
   174→    test_dirs = lang.external_test_dirs or ["tests", "test"]
   175→    exts = tuple(lang.test_file_extensions or lang.extensions)
   176→    for test_dir in test_dirs:
   177→        d = project_root / test_dir
   178→        if not d.is_dir():
   179→            continue
   180→        if d.resolve().is_relative_to(path_root):
   181→            continue  # test_dir is inside scanned path, zone_map already has it
   182→        for root, _, files in os.walk(d):
   183→            for filename in files:
   184→                if any(filename.endswith(ext) for ext in exts):
   185→                    extra.add(os.path.join(root, filename))
   186→    return extra
   187→
   188→
   189→def _entries_to_findings(
   190→    detector: str,
   191→    entries: list[dict],
   192→    *,
   193→    default_name: str = "",
   194→    include_zone: bool = False,
   195→    zone_map=None,
   196→) -> list[Finding]:
   197→    """Convert detector entries to normalized findings."""
   198→    results: list[Finding] = []
   199→    for entry in entries:
   200→        finding = make_finding(
   201→            detector,
   202→            entry["file"],
   203→            entry.get("name", default_name),
   204→            tier=entry["tier"],
   205→            confidence=entry["confidence"],
   206→            summary=entry["summary"],
   207→            detail=entry.get("detail", {}),
   208→        )
   209→        if include_zone and zone_map is not None:
   210→            z = zone_map.get(entry["file"])
   211→            if z is not None:
   212→                finding["zone"] = z.value
   213→        results.append(finding)
   214→    return results
   215→
   216→
   217→def _log_phase_summary(label: str, results: list[Finding], potential: int, unit: str) -> None:
   218→    """Emit standardized shared-phase summary logging."""
   219→    if results:
   220→        log(f"         {label}: {len(results)} findings ({potential} {unit})")
   221→    else:
   222→        log(f"         {label}: clean ({potential} {unit})")
   223→
   224→
   225→def _coverage_to_dict(coverage: DetectorCoverageStatus) -> dict[str, object]:
   226→    return {
   227→        "detector": coverage.detector,
   228→        "status": coverage.status,
   229→        "confidence": round(coerce_confidence(coverage.confidence), 2),
   230→        "summary": coverage.summary,
   231→        "impact": coverage.impact,
   232→        "remediation": coverage.remediation,
   233→        "tool": coverage.tool,
   234→        "reason": coverage.reason,
   235→    }
   236→
   237→
   238→def _merge_detector_coverage(
   239→    existing: dict[str, object],
   240→    incoming: dict[str, object],
   241→) -> dict[str, object]:
   242→    merged = dict(existing)
   243→    merged["status"] = (
   244→        "reduced"
   245→        if str(existing.get("status", "full")) == "reduced"
   246→        or str(incoming.get("status", "full")) == "reduced"
   247→        else "full"
   248→    )
   249→    merged["confidence"] = round(
   250→        min(
   251→            coerce_confidence(existing.get("confidence")),
   252→            coerce_confidence(incoming.get("confidence")),
   253→        ),
   254→        2,
   255→    )
   256→
   257→    for key in ("summary", "impact", "remediation", "tool", "reason"):
   258→        current = str(merged.get(key, "") or "").strip()
   259→        update = str(incoming.get(key, "") or "").strip()
   260→        if update and not current:
   261→            merged[key] = update
   262→        elif update and current and update not in current:
   263→            merged[key] = f"{current} | {update}"
   264→    return merged
   265→
   266→
   267→def _record_detector_coverage(lang: LangRuntimeContract, coverage: DetectorCoverageStatus | None) -> None:
   268→    if coverage is None:
   269→        return
   270→    normalized = _coverage_to_dict(coverage)
   271→    detector = str(normalized.get("detector", "")).strip()
   272→    if not detector:
   273→        return
   274→    existing = lang.detector_coverage.get(detector)
   275→    if isinstance(existing, dict):
   276→        lang.detector_coverage[detector] = _merge_detector_coverage(existing, normalized)
   277→    else:
   278→        lang.detector_coverage[detector] = normalized
   279→
   280→
   281→def phase_security(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   282→    """Shared phase: detect security issues (cross-language + lang-specific)."""
   283→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   284→    files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
   285→    entries, cross_lang_scanned = detect_security_issues(
   286→        files,
   287→        zone_map,
   288→        lang.name,
   289→        scan_root=path,
   290→    )
   291→    lang_scanned = 0
   292→
   293→    # Also call lang-specific security detectors.
   294→    lang_result = lang.detect_lang_security_detailed(files, zone_map)
   295→    lang_entries = lang_result.entries
   296→    lang_scanned = max(0, int(lang_result.files_scanned))
   297→    _record_detector_coverage(lang, lang_result.coverage)
   298→    entries.extend(lang_entries)
   299→
   300→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "security")
   301→    potential = max(cross_lang_scanned, lang_scanned)
   302→
   303→    results = _entries_to_findings(
   304→        "security",
   305→        entries,
   306→        include_zone=True,
   307→        zone_map=zone_map,
   308→    )
   309→    _log_phase_summary("security", results, potential, "files scanned")
   310→
   311→    if "security" not in lang.detector_coverage:
   312→        lang.detector_coverage["security"] = {
   313→            "detector": "security",
   314→            "status": "full",
   315→            "confidence": 1.0,
   316→            "summary": "Security coverage complete for enabled detectors.",
   317→            "impact": "",
   318→            "remediation": "",
   319→            "tool": "",
   320→            "reason": "",
   321→        }
   322→
   323→    return results, {"security": potential}
   324→
   325→
   326→def phase_test_coverage(
   327→    path: Path,
   328→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   329→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   330→    """Shared phase: detect test coverage gaps."""
   331→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   332→    if zone_map is None:
   333→        return [], {}
   334→
   335→    graph = lang.dep_graph or lang.build_dep_graph(path)
   336→    extra = find_external_test_files(path, lang)
   337→    entries, potential = detect_test_coverage(
   338→        graph,
   339→        zone_map,
   340→        lang.name,
   341→        extra_test_files=extra or None,
   342→        complexity_map=lang.complexity_map or None,
   343→    )
   344→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "test_coverage")
   345→
   346→    results = _entries_to_findings("test_coverage", entries, default_name="")
   347→    _log_phase_summary("test coverage", results, potential, "production files")
   348→
   349→    return results, {"test_coverage": potential}
   350→
   351→
   352→def phase_private_imports(
   353→    path: Path,
   354→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   355→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   356→    """Shared phase: detect cross-module private imports."""
   357→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   358→    graph = lang.dep_graph or lang.build_dep_graph(path)
   359→
   360→    entries, potential = lang.detect_private_imports(graph, zone_map)
   361→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "private_imports")
   362→
   363→    results = _entries_to_findings("private_imports", entries)
   364→    _log_phase_summary("private imports", results, potential, "files scanned")
   365→
   366→    return results, {"private_imports": potential}
   367→
   368→
   369→def phase_subjective_review(
   370→    path: Path,
   371→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   372→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   373→    """Shared phase: detect files missing subjective design review."""
   374→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   375→    max_age = lang.review_max_age_days
   376→    files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
   377→    review_cache = lang.review_cache
   378→    if isinstance(review_cache, dict) and "files" in review_cache:
   379→        per_file_cache = review_cache.get("files", {})
   380→    else:
   381→        # Legacy format: flat dict of file entries with no "files" wrapper.
   382→        # Filter out known top-level structural keys so they aren't treated as
   383→        # file paths, then reconstruct the canonical shape preserving them.
   384→        _TOP_LEVEL_KEYS = frozenset({"holistic"})
   385→        raw = review_cache if isinstance(review_cache, dict) else {}
   386→        per_file_cache = {k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k not in _TOP_LEVEL_KEYS}
   387→        review_cache = {"files": per_file_cache}
   388→        if "holistic" in raw:
   389→            review_cache["holistic"] = raw["holistic"]
   390→
   391→    entries, potential = detect_review_coverage(
   392→        files,
   393→        zone_map,
   394→        per_file_cache,
   395→        lang.name,
   396→        low_value_pattern=lang.review_low_value_pattern,
   397→        max_age_days=max_age,
   398→        holistic_cache=review_cache.get("holistic")
   399→        if isinstance(review_cache, dict)
   400→        else None,
   401→        holistic_total_files=len(files),
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    # Also check holistic review staleness.
   405→    holistic_entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(
   406→        review_cache,
   407→        total_files=len(files),
   408→        max_age_days=max_age,
   409→    )
   410→    entries.extend(holistic_entries)
   411→
   412→    results = _entries_to_findings("subjective_review", entries)
   413→    _log_phase_summary("subjective review", results, potential, "reviewable files")
   414→
   415→    return results, {"subjective_review": potential}
   416→
   417→
   418→def phase_signature(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   419→    """Shared phase runner: detect signature variance via lang.extract_functions.
   420→
   421→    Backend-agnostic — works with any extractor that returns FunctionInfo objects
   422→    (tree-sitter, regex, AST, etc.).  Returns empty results when the lang has no
   423→    function extractor.
   424→    """
   425→    from desloppify.engine.detectors.signature import detect_signature_variance
   426→
   427→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path)
   428→
   429→    findings: list[Finding] = []
   430→    potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
   431→
   432→    if not functions:
   433→        return findings, potentials
   434→
   435→    entries, _total = detect_signature_variance(functions, min_occurrences=3)
   436→    for e in entries:
   437→        findings.append(make_finding(
   438→            "signature", e["files"][0],
   439→            f"signature_variance::{e['name']}",
   440→            tier=3, confidence="medium",
   441→            summary=(
   442→                f"'{e['name']}' has {e['signature_count']} different signatures "
   443→                f"across {e['file_count']} files"
   444→            ),
   445→        ))
   446→    if entries:
   447→        potentials["signature"] = len(entries)
   448→        log(f"         signature variance: {len(entries)}")
   449→
   450→    return findings, potentials
   451→
   452→
   453→def run_structural_phase(
   454→    path: Path,
   455→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   456→    *,
   457→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
   458→    log_fn,
   459→    min_loc: int = 40,
   460→    god_rules=None,
   461→    god_extractor_fn=None,
   462→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   463→    """Run large/complexity/flat directory detectors for a language.
   464→
   465→    Optional ``god_rules`` + ``god_extractor_fn`` enable god-class detection:
   466→    when both are provided, ``god_extractor_fn(path)`` is called to extract
   467→    class info, then ``detect_gods()`` finds classes matching multiple rules.
   468→    """
   469→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
   470→
   471→    large_entries, file_count = detect_large_files(
   472→        path,
   473→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   474→        threshold=lang.large_threshold,
   475→    )
   476→    for entry in large_entries:
   477→        add_structural_signal(
   478→            structural,
   479→            entry["file"],
   480→            f"large ({entry['loc']} LOC)",
   481→            {"loc": entry["loc"]},
   482→        )
   483→
   484→    complexity_entries, _ = detect_complexity(
   485→        path,
   486→        signals=complexity_signals,
   487→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   488→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
   489→        min_loc=min_loc,
   490→    )
   491→    for entry in complexity_entries:
   492→        add_structural_signal(
   493→            structural,
   494→            entry["file"],
   495→            f"complexity score {entry['score']}",
   496→            {"complexity_score": entry["score"], "complexity_signals": entry["signals"]},
   497→        )
   498→        lang.complexity_map[entry["file"]] = entry["score"]
   499→
   500→    if god_rules and god_extractor_fn:
   501→        from desloppify.engine.detectors.gods import detect_gods
   502→
   503→        god_entries, _ = detect_gods(god_extractor_fn(path), god_rules, min_reasons=2)
   504→        for entry in god_entries:
   505→            add_structural_signal(
   506→                structural, entry["file"], entry["signal_text"], entry["detail"],
   507→            )
   508→        if god_entries:
   509→            log_fn(f"         god classes: {len(god_entries)}")
   510→
   511→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log_fn)
   512→    flat_entries, analyzed_dir_count = detect_flat_dirs(path, file_finder=lang.file_finder)
   513→    for entry in flat_entries:
   514→        child_dir_count = int(entry.get("child_dir_count", 0))
   515→        combined_score = int(entry.get("combined_score", entry.get("file_count", 0)))
   516→        results.append(
   517→            make_finding(
   518→                "flat_dirs",
   519→                entry["directory"],
   520→                "",
   521→                tier=3,
   522→                confidence="medium",
   523→                summary=format_flat_dir_summary(entry),
   524→                detail={
   525→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
   526→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   527→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   528→                    "kind": entry.get("kind", "overload"),
   529→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(entry.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   530→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(entry.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   531→                    "sparse_child_count": int(entry.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   532→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(entry.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   533→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   534→                        entry.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   535→                    ),
   536→                },
   537→            )
   538→        )
   539→    if flat_entries:
   540→        log_fn(
   541→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   542→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   543→        )
   544→
   545→    potentials = {
   546→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   547→        "flat_dirs": analyzed_dir_count,
   548→    }
   549→    return results, potentials
   550→
   551→
   552→def run_coupling_phase(
   553→    path: Path,
   554→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   555→    *,
   556→    build_dep_graph_fn,
   557→    log_fn,
   558→    post_process_fn=None,
   559→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   560→    """Run single-use/cycles/orphaned detectors against a language dep graph.
   561→
   562→    Optional ``post_process_fn(findings, entries, lang)`` is called after
   563→    creating single-use and orphaned findings to allow per-language
   564→    adjustments (e.g. confidence gating based on corroboration signals).
   565→    """
   566→    graph = build_dep_graph_fn(path)
   567→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   568→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   569→    results: list[dict] = []
   570→
   571→    single_entries, single_candidates = detect_single_use_abstractions(
   572→        path,
   573→        graph,
   574→        barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   575→    )
   576→    single_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, single_entries, "single_use")
   577→    single_findings = make_single_use_findings(
   578→        single_entries, lang.get_area, stderr_fn=log_fn,
   579→    )
   580→    if post_process_fn:
   581→        post_process_fn(single_findings, single_entries, lang)
   582→    results.extend(single_findings)
   583→
   584→    cycle_entries, _ = detect_cycles(graph)
   585→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   586→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log_fn))
   587→
   588→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = detect_orphaned_files(
   589→        path,
   590→        graph,
   591→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   592→        options=OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   593→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   594→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   595→        ),
   596→    )
   597→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   598→    orphan_findings = make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log_fn)
   599→    if post_process_fn:
   600→        post_process_fn(orphan_findings, orphan_entries, lang)
   601→    results.extend(orphan_findings)
   602→
   603→    log_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   604→    potentials = {
   605→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zone_map, single_candidates),
   606→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   607→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   608→    }
   609→    return results, potentials
   610→
   611→
   612→def make_structural_coupling_phase_pair(
   613→    *,
   614→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
   615→    build_dep_graph_fn,
   616→    log_fn,
   617→) -> tuple:
   618→    """Create default structural/coupling phase callables for a language.
   619→
   620→    This keeps language phase modules declarative by letting them provide only
   621→    their complexity signals and dependency-graph builder.
   622→    """
   623→
   624→    def phase_structural(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   625→        return run_structural_phase(
   626→            path,
   627→            lang,
   628→            complexity_signals=complexity_signals,
   629→            log_fn=log_fn,
   630→        )
   631→
   632→    def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   633→        return run_coupling_phase(
   634→            path,
   635→            lang,
   636→            build_dep_graph_fn=build_dep_graph_fn,
   637→            log_fn=log_fn,
   638→        )
   639→
   640→    return phase_structural, phase_coupling
   641→
   642→
   643→__all__ = [
   644→    "find_external_test_files",
   645→    "make_structural_coupling_phase_pair",
   646→    "phase_boilerplate_duplication",
   647→    "phase_dupes",
   648→    "phase_private_imports",
   649→    "phase_security",
   650→    "phase_signature",
   651→    "phase_subjective_review",
   652→    "phase_test_coverage",
   653→    "run_coupling_phase",
   654→    "run_structural_phase",
   655→]
   656→
```

```
    61→def _compute_ts_destructure_props(content, lines):
    62→    long_destructures = re.findall(r"\{\s*(\w+(?:\s*,\s*\w+){8,})\s*\}", content)
    63→    if not long_destructures:
    64→        return None
    65→    max_props = max(len(d.split(",")) for d in long_destructures)
    66→    return max_props, f"destructure w/{max_props} props"

    69→def _compute_ts_inline_types(content, lines):
    70→    inline_types = len(

    72→    )
    73→    if inline_types > 3:
    74→        return inline_types, f"{inline_types} inline types"
    75→    return None

   138→def phase_logs(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:

   145→    results = []
   146→    for (file, tag), entries in log_groups.items():
   147→        results.append(
   148→            make_finding(
   149→                "logs",
   150→                file,
   151→                tag,
   152→                tier=1,
   153→                confidence="high",
   154→                summary=f"{len(entries)} tagged logs [{tag}]",
   155→                detail={

   158→                },

   199→        if e["kind"] == "property":
   200→            continue
   201→        tier = 1 if e["importers"] == 0 else 3
   202→        results.append(
   203→            make_finding(
   204→                "deprecated",
   205→                e["file"],
   206→                e["symbol"],
   207→                tier=tier,
   208→                confidence="high",
   209→                summary=f"Deprecated: {e['symbol']} ({e['importers']} importers)"
   210→                + (" → safe to delete" if e["importers"] == 0 else ""),
   211→                detail={"importers": e["importers"], "line": e["line"]},
   212→            )
   213→        )
   214→    log(
   215→        f"         {len(dep_entries)} instances → {len(results)} findings (properties suppressed)"
   216→    )
   217→    return results, {"deprecated": total_deprecated}
   218→
   219→
   220→def phase_structural(
   221→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   222→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   223→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
   224→
   225→    large_entries, file_count = large_detector_mod.detect_large_files(
   226→        path,
   227→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   228→        threshold=lang.large_threshold,
   229→    )
   230→    for e in large_entries:
   231→        add_structural_signal(
   232→            structural, e["file"], f"large ({e['loc']} LOC)", {"loc": e["loc"]}
   233→        )
   234→
   235→    complexity_entries, _ = complexity_detector_mod.detect_complexity(
   236→        path,
   237→        signals=TS_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS,
   238→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   239→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
   240→    )
   241→    for e in complexity_entries:
   242→        add_structural_signal(
   243→            structural,
   244→            e["file"],
   245→            f"complexity score {e['score']}",
   246→            {"complexity_score": e["score"], "complexity_signals": e["signals"]},
   247→        )
   248→        lang.complexity_map[e["file"]] = e["score"]
   249→
   250→    god_entries, _ = gods_detector_mod.detect_gods(
   251→        extract_ts_components(path), TS_GOD_RULES, min_reasons=2
   252→    )
   253→    for e in god_entries:
   254→        add_structural_signal(
   255→            structural,
   256→            e["file"],
   257→            f"{e['detail'].get('hook_total', 0)} hooks ({', '.join(e['reasons'][:2])})",
   258→            {
   259→                "hook_total": e["detail"].get("hook_total", 0),
   260→                "hook_reasons": e["reasons"],
   261→            },
   262→        )
   263→
   264→    concern_entries, _ = concerns_detector_mod.detect_mixed_concerns(path)
   265→    for e in concern_entries:
   266→        add_structural_signal(
   267→            structural,
   268→            e["file"],
   269→            f"mixed: {', '.join(e['concerns'][:3])}",
   270→            {"concerns": e["concerns"]},
   271→        )
   272→
   273→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log)
   274→
   275→    # Flat directories (too many files → missing sub-organization)
   276→    flat_entries, dir_count = flat_dirs_detector_mod.detect_flat_dirs(
   277→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder
   278→    )
   279→    for e in flat_entries:
   280→        child_dir_count = int(e.get("child_dir_count", 0))
   281→        combined_score = int(e.get("combined_score", e.get("file_count", 0)))
   282→        results.append(
   283→            make_finding(
   284→                "flat_dirs",
   285→                e["directory"],
   286→                "",
   287→                tier=3,
   288→                confidence="medium",
   289→                summary=flat_dirs_detector_mod.format_flat_dir_summary(e),
   290→                detail={
   291→                    "file_count": e["file_count"],
   292→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   293→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   294→                    "kind": e.get("kind", "overload"),
   295→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(e.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   296→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(e.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   297→                    "sparse_child_count": int(e.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   298→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(e.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   299→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   300→                        e.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   301→                    ),
   302→                },
   303→            )
   304→        )
   305→    if flat_entries:
   306→        log(
   307→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   308→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   309→        )
   310→
   311→    # TS-specific: props bloat
   312→    props_thresh = lang.props_threshold
   313→    prop_entries, prop_count = props_detector_mod.detect_prop_interface_bloat(
   314→        path, threshold=props_thresh
   315→    )
   316→    for e in prop_entries:
   317→        pc = e["prop_count"]
   318→        # Tiered severity: 15-29=low, 30-49=medium, 50+=high
   319→        if pc >= 50:
   320→            conf, tier = "high", 4
   321→        elif pc >= 30:
   322→            conf, tier = "medium", 3
   323→        else:
   324→            conf, tier = "low", 3
   325→        results.append(
   326→            make_finding(
   327→                "props",
   328→                e["file"],
   329→                e["interface"],
   330→                tier=tier,
   331→                confidence=conf,
   332→                summary=f"Bloated {e.get('kind', 'props')}: {e['interface']} ({pc} fields)",
   333→                detail={
   334→                    "prop_count": pc,
   335→                    "line": e["line"],
   336→                    "kind": e.get("kind", "props"),
   337→                },
   338→            )
   339→        )
   340→
   341→    # TS-specific: passthrough components
   342→    pt_entries = detect_passthrough_components(path)
   343→    for e in pt_entries:
   344→        results.append(
   345→            make_finding(
   346→                "props",
   347→                e["file"],
   348→                f"passthrough::{e['component']}",
   349→                tier=e["tier"],
   350→                confidence=e["confidence"],
   351→                summary=f"Passthrough component: {e['component']} "
   352→                f"({e['passthrough']}/{e['total_props']} props forwarded, {e['ratio']:.0%})",
   353→                detail={
   354→                    "passthrough": e["passthrough"],
   355→                    "total_props": e["total_props"],
   356→                    "ratio": e["ratio"],
   357→                    "line": e["line"],
   358→                    "passthrough_props": e["passthrough_props"],
   359→                    "direct_props": e["direct_props"],
   360→                },
   361→            )
   362→        )
   363→    potentials = {
   364→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   365→        "flat_dirs": dir_count,
   366→        "props": max(prop_count, len(pt_entries)) if prop_count else len(pt_entries),
   367→    }
   368→    return results, potentials
   369→
   370→
   371→def _make_boundary_findings(
   372→    single_entries: list[dict],
   373→    path: Path,
   374→    graph: dict,
   375→    lang: LangRun,
   376→    shared_prefix: str,
   377→    tools_prefix: str,
   378→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   379→    """Create boundary-candidate findings, deduplicated against single-use."""
   380→    single_use_emitted = set()
   381→    for e in single_entries:
   382→        is_size_ok = 50 <= e["loc"] <= 200
   383→        is_colocated = lang.get_area and (
   384→            lang.get_area(rel(e["file"])) == lang.get_area(e["sole_importer"])
   385→        )
   386→        if not is_size_ok and not is_colocated:
   387→            single_use_emitted.add(rel(e["file"]))
   388→
   389→    results = []
   390→    deduped = 0
   391→    boundary_entries, total_shared = coupling_detector_mod.detect_boundary_candidates(
   392→        path,
   393→        graph,
   394→        shared_prefix=shared_prefix,
   395→        tools_prefix=tools_prefix,
   396→        skip_basenames={"index.ts", "index.tsx"},
   397→    )
   398→    for e in boundary_entries:
   399→        if rel(e["file"]) in single_use_emitted:
   400→            deduped += 1
   401→            continue
   402→        results.append(
   403→            make_finding(
   404→                "coupling",
   405→                e["file"],
   406→                f"boundary::{e['sole_tool']}",
   407→                tier=3,
   408→                confidence="medium",
   409→                summary=f"Boundary candidate ({e['loc']} LOC): only used by {e['sole_tool']} "
   410→                f"({e['importer_count']} importers)",
   411→                detail={
   412→                    "sole_tool": e["sole_tool"],
   413→                    "importer_count": e["importer_count"],
   414→                    "loc": e["loc"],
   415→                },
   416→            )
   417→        )
   418→    if deduped:
   419→        log(f"         ({deduped} boundary candidates skipped — covered by single_use)")
   420→    return results, total_shared
   421→
   422→
   423→def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   424→    results = []
   425→    graph = deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph(path)
   426→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   427→    zm = lang.zone_map
   428→
   429→    # Single-use (shared helper)
   430→    single_entries, single_candidates = (
   431→        single_use_detector_mod.detect_single_use_abstractions(
   432→            path, graph, barrel_names=lang.barrel_names
   433→        )
   434→    )
   435→    single_entries = filter_entries(zm, single_entries, "single_use")
   436→    results.extend(
   437→        make_single_use_findings(
   438→            single_entries, lang.get_area, skip_dir_names={"commands"}, stderr_fn=log
   439→        )
   440→    )
   441→    src_path = get_src_path()
   442→    shared_prefix = f"{src_path}/shared/"
   443→    tools_prefix = f"{src_path}/tools/"
   444→    coupling_entries, coupling_edge_counts = coupling_detector_mod.detect_coupling_violations(
   445→        path, graph, shared_prefix=shared_prefix, tools_prefix=tools_prefix
   446→    )
   447→    coupling_entries = filter_entries(zm, coupling_entries, "coupling")
   448→    for e in coupling_entries:
   449→        results.append(
   450→            make_finding(
   451→                "coupling",
   452→                e["file"],
   453→                e["target"],
   454→                tier=2,
   455→                confidence="high",
   456→                summary=f"Backwards coupling: shared imports {e['target']} (tool: {e['tool']})",
   457→                detail={
   458→                    "target": e["target"],
   459→                    "tool": e["tool"],
   460→                    "direction": e["direction"],
   461→                },
   462→            )
   463→        )
   464→
   465→    # TS-specific: boundary candidates (deduplicated against single-use)
   466→    boundary_findings, _ = _make_boundary_findings(
   467→        single_entries, path, graph, lang, shared_prefix, tools_prefix
   468→    )
   469→    results.extend(boundary_findings)
   470→
   471→    # TS-specific: cross-tool imports
   472→    cross_tool, cross_edge_counts = coupling_detector_mod.detect_cross_tool_imports(
   473→        path, graph, tools_prefix=tools_prefix
   474→    )
   475→    cross_tool = filter_entries(zm, cross_tool, "coupling")
   476→    for e in cross_tool:
   477→        results.append(
   478→            make_finding(
   479→                "coupling",
   480→                e["file"],
   481→                e["target"],
   482→                tier=2,
   483→                confidence="high",
   484→                summary=f"Cross-tool import: {e['source_tool']}→{e['target_tool']} ({e['target']})",
   485→                detail={
   486→                    "target": e["target"],
   487→                    "source_tool": e["source_tool"],
   488→                    "target_tool": e["target_tool"],
   489→                    "direction": e["direction"],
   490→                },
   491→            )
   492→        )
   493→    if cross_tool:
   494→        log(f"         cross-tool: {len(cross_tool)} imports")
   495→
   496→    # Cycles + orphaned (shared helpers)
   497→    cycle_entries, _ = graph_detector_mod.detect_cycles(graph)
   498→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zm, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   499→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log))
   500→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = orphaned_detector_mod.detect_orphaned_files(
   501→        path,
   502→        graph,
   503→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   504→        options=orphaned_detector_mod.OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   505→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   506→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   507→            dynamic_import_finder=deps_detector_mod.build_dynamic_import_targets,
   508→            alias_resolver=deps_detector_mod.ts_alias_resolver,
   509→        ),
   510→    )
   511→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zm, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   512→    results.extend(make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log))
   513→
   514→    # Re-export facades (shared detector)
   515→    facade_entries, _ = facade_detector_mod.detect_reexport_facades(graph)
   516→    facade_entries = filter_entries(zm, facade_entries, "facade")
   517→    results.extend(make_facade_findings(facade_entries, log))
   518→
   519→    # TS-specific: pattern consistency
   520→    pattern_result = patterns_detector_mod.detect_pattern_anomalies_result(path)
   521→    pattern_entries = pattern_result.entries
   522→    total_areas = pattern_result.population_size
   523→    for e in pattern_entries:
   524→        results.append(
   525→            make_finding(
   526→                "patterns",
   527→                e["area"],
   528→                e["family"],
   529→                tier=3,
   530→                confidence=e.get("confidence", "low"),
   531→                summary=f"Competing patterns ({e['family']}): {e['review'][:120]}",
   532→                detail={
   533→                    "family": e["family"],
   534→                    "patterns_used": e["patterns_used"],
   535→                    "pattern_count": e["pattern_count"],
   536→                    "review": e["review"],
   537→                },
   538→            )
   539→        )
   540→
   541→    # TS-specific: naming consistency
   542→    naming_entries, total_dirs = naming_detector_mod.detect_naming_inconsistencies(
   543→        path,
   544→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   545→        skip_names=TS_SKIP_NAMES,
   546→        skip_dirs=TS_SKIP_DIRS,
   547→    )
   548→    for e in naming_entries:
   549→        results.append(
   550→            make_finding(
   551→                "naming",
   552→                e["directory"],
   553→                e["minority"],
   554→                tier=3,
   555→                confidence="low",
   556→                summary=f"Naming inconsistency: {e['minority_count']} {e['minority']} files "
   557→                f"in {e['majority']}-majority dir ({e['total_files']} total)",
   558→                detail={
   559→                    "majority": e["majority"],
   560→                    "majority_count": e["majority_count"],
   561→                    "minority": e["minority"],
   562→                    "minority_count": e["minority_count"],
   563→                    "outliers": e["outliers"],
   564→                },
   565→            )
   566→        )
   567→    log(f"         → {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   568→    coupling_edges = coupling_edge_counts.eligible_edges
   569→    cross_edges = cross_edge_counts.eligible_edges
   570→    potentials = {
   571→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zm, single_candidates),
   572→        "coupling": coupling_edges + cross_edges,
   573→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   574→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   575→        "patterns": total_areas,
   576→        "naming": total_dirs,
   577→        "facade": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   578→    }
   579→    return results, potentials
   580→
   581→
   582→def phase_smells(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   583→    smell_entries, total_smell_files = smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)
   584→    results = make_smell_findings(smell_entries, log)
   585→
   586→    # Cross-file: signature variance
   587→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path) if lang.extract_functions else []
   588→    sig_entries, _ = signature_detector_mod.detect_signature_variance(functions)
   589→    for e in sig_entries:
   590→        results.append(
   591→            make_finding(
   592→                "smells",
   593→                e["files"][0],
   594→                f"sig_variance::{e['name']}",
   595→                tier=3,
   596→                confidence="medium",
   597→                summary=f"Signature variance: {e['name']}() has {e['signature_count']} "
   598→                f"different signatures across {e['file_count']} files",
   599→                detail={
   600→                    "function": e["name"],
   601→                    "file_count": e["file_count"],
   602→                    "signature_count": e["signature_count"],
   603→                    "variants": e["variants"][:5],
   604→                },
   605→            )
   606→        )
   607→    if sig_entries:
   608→        log(
   609→            f"         signature variance: {len(sig_entries)} functions with inconsistent signatures"
   610→        )
   611→
   612→    # TS-specific: React state sync anti-patterns
   613→    react_entries, total_effects = react_detector_mod.detect_state_sync(path)
   614→    for e in react_entries:
   615→        setter_str = ", ".join(e["setters"])
   616→        results.append(
   617→            make_finding(
   618→                "react",
   619→                e["file"],
   620→                setter_str,
   621→                tier=3,
   622→                confidence="medium",
   623→                summary=f"State sync anti-pattern: useEffect only calls {setter_str}",
   624→                detail={"line": e["line"], "setters": e["setters"]},
   625→            )
   626→        )
   627→    if react_entries:
   628→        log(f"         react: {len(react_entries)} state sync anti-patterns")
   629→
   630→    # TS-specific: Context provider nesting depth
   631→    nesting_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_context_nesting(path)
   632→    for e in nesting_entries:
   633→        providers_str = " → ".join(e["providers"][:5])
   634→        results.append(
   635→            make_finding(
   636→                "react",
   637→                e["file"],
   638→                f"nesting::{e['depth']}",
   639→                tier=3,
   640→                confidence="medium",
   641→                summary=f"Deep provider nesting ({e['depth']} levels): {providers_str}",
   642→                detail={"depth": e["depth"], "providers": e["providers"]},
   643→            )
   644→        )
   645→    if nesting_entries:
   646→        log(f"         react: {len(nesting_entries)} deep provider nesting")
   647→
   648→    # TS-specific: Hook return bloat
   649→    hook_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_hook_return_bloat(path)
   650→    for e in hook_entries:
   651→        results.append(
   652→            make_finding(
   653→                "react",
   654→                e["file"],
   655→                f"hook_bloat::{e['hook']}",
   656→                tier=3,
   657→                confidence="medium",
   658→                summary=f"Hook return bloat: {e['hook']} returns {e['field_count']} fields",
   659→                detail={
   660→                    "hook": e["hook"],
   661→                    "field_count": e["field_count"],
   662→                    "line": e["line"],
   663→                },
   664→            )
   665→        )
   666→    if hook_entries:
   667→        log(f"         react: {len(hook_entries)} bloated hook returns")
   668→
   669→    # TS-specific: Boolean state explosion
   670→    bool_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_boolean_state_explosion(path)
   671→    for e in bool_entries:
   672→        states_str = ", ".join(e["states"][:5])
   673→        results.append(
   674→            make_finding(
   675→                "react",
   676→                e["file"],
   677→                f"bool_state::{e['prefix']}",
   678→                tier=3,
   679→                confidence="low",
   680→                summary=f"Boolean state explosion: {e['count']} boolean useState hooks ({states_str})",
   681→                detail={
   682→                    "count": e["count"],
   683→                    "setters": e["setters"],
   684→                    "states": e["states"],
   685→                    "line": e["line"],
   686→                },
   687→            )
   688→        )
   689→    if bool_entries:
   690→        log(f"         react: {len(bool_entries)} boolean state explosions")
   691→
   692→    return results, {
   693→        "smells": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_smell_files),
   694→        "react": total_effects,
   695→    }
   696→
   697→
   698→# Backward-compatible aliases for internal callers/tests.
   699→_phase_logs = phase_logs
   700→_phase_unused = phase_unused
   701→_phase_exports = phase_exports
   702→_phase_deprecated = phase_deprecated
   703→_phase_structural = phase_structural
   704→_phase_coupling = phase_coupling
   705→_phase_smells = phase_smells
   706→
```

```
    52→    ComplexitySignal(
    53→        "nested_comprehensions",
    54→        r"\[[^\]]*\bfor\b[^\]]*\bfor\b[^\]]*\]|\{[^}]*\bfor\b[^}]*\bfor\b[^}]*\}",
    55→        weight=2,
    56→        threshold=2,
    57→    ),
    58→    ComplexitySignal("TODOs", r"#\s*(?:TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX)", weight=2, threshold=0),
    59→]
    60→
    61→PY_GOD_RULES = [
    62→    GodRule("methods", "methods", lambda c: len(c.methods), 15),
    63→    GodRule("attributes", "attributes", lambda c: len(c.attributes), 10),
    64→    GodRule("base_classes", "base classes", lambda c: len(c.base_classes), 3),
    65→    GodRule(
    66→        "long_methods",
    67→        "long methods (>50 LOC)",
    68→        lambda c: sum(1 for m in c.methods if m.loc > 50),
    69→        1,
    70→    ),
    71→]
    72→
    73→PY_SKIP_NAMES = {
    74→    "__init__.py",
    75→    "conftest.py",
    76→    "setup.py",
    77→    "manage.py",
    78→    "__main__.py",
    79→    "wsgi.py",
    80→    "asgi.py",
    81→}
    82→
    83→PY_ENTRY_PATTERNS = [
    84→    "__main__.py",
    85→    "conftest.py",
    86→    "manage.py",
    87→    "setup.py",
    88→    "setup.cfg",
    89→    "test_",
    90→    "_test.py",
    91→    ".test.",
    92→    "/tests/",
    93→    "/test/",
    94→    "/migrations/",
    95→    "settings.py",
    96→    "config.py",
    97→    "wsgi.py",
    98→    "asgi.py",
    99→    "cli.py",  # CLI entry points (loaded via framework/importlib)
   100→    "/commands/",  # CLI subcommands (loaded dynamically)
   101→    "/fixers/",  # Fixer modules (loaded dynamically)
   102→    "/lang/",  # Language modules (loaded dynamically)
   103→    "/extractors/",  # Extractor modules (loaded dynamically)
   104→    "__init__.py",  # Package init files (barrels, not orphans)
   105→]
   106→
   107→
   108→def phase_unused(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   109→    entries, total_files = unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path)
   110→    return make_unused_findings(entries, log), {
   111→        "unused": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
   112→    }
   113→
   114→
   115→def phase_structural(
   116→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   117→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   118→    return run_phase_structural(
   119→        path,
   120→        lang,
   121→        complexity_signals=PY_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS,
   122→        god_rules=PY_GOD_RULES,
   123→        log_fn=log,
   124→    )
   125→
   126→
   127→def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   128→    return run_phase_coupling(path, lang, log_fn=log)
   129→
   130→
   131→def phase_responsibility_cohesion(
   132→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   133→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   134→    entries, candidates = cohesion_detector_mod.detect_responsibility_cohesion(path)
   135→    entries = filter_entries(lang.zone_map, entries, "responsibility_cohesion")
   136→
   137→    results: list[dict] = []
   138→    for entry in entries:
   139→        comp_sizes = ", ".join(str(size) for size in entry["component_sizes"][:5])
   140→        if len(entry["component_sizes"]) > 5:
   141→            comp_sizes += f", +{len(entry['component_sizes']) - 5} more"
   142→        results.append(
   143→            state_mod.make_finding(
   144→                "responsibility_cohesion",
   145→                entry["file"],
   146→                "",
   147→                tier=3,
   148→                confidence="medium",
   149→                summary=(
   150→                    f"Mixed responsibilities: {entry['function_count']} top-level funcs "
   151→                    f"across {entry['component_count']} disconnected clusters "
   152→                    f"({comp_sizes})"
   153→                ),
   154→                detail={
   155→                    "loc": entry["loc"],
   156→                    "function_count": entry["function_count"],
   157→                    "component_count": entry["component_count"],
   158→                    "component_sizes": entry["component_sizes"],
   159→                    "family_count": entry["family_count"],
   160→                    "import_cluster_count": entry["import_cluster_count"],
   161→                    "families": entry["families"],
   162→                },
   163→            )
   164→        )
   165→
   166→    if results:
   167→        log(f"         responsibility cohesion: {len(results)} modules")
   168→    return results, {
   169→        "responsibility_cohesion": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, candidates)
   170→    }
   171→
   172→def phase_uncalled_functions(
   173→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   174→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   175→    """Detect underscore-prefixed top-level functions with zero references."""
   176→    entries, total = uncalled_detector_mod.detect_uncalled_functions(
   177→        path, lang.dep_graph
   178→    )
   179→    zm = lang.zone_map
   180→    entries = filter_entries(zm, entries, "uncalled_functions")
   181→
   182→    results: list[Finding] = []
   183→    for entry in entries:
   184→        results.append(
   185→            state_mod.make_finding(
   186→                "uncalled_functions",
   187→                entry["file"],
   188→                entry["name"],
   189→                tier=3,
   190→                confidence="high",
   191→                summary=f"Uncalled private function: {entry['name']}() — {entry['loc']} LOC, zero references",
   192→                detail={"line": entry["line"], "loc": entry["loc"]},
   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→
   196→    if results:
   197→        log(f"         uncalled functions: {len(results)} dead private functions")
   198→    return results, {"uncalled_functions": adjust_potential(zm, total)}
   199→
   200→
   201→# Backward-compatible aliases for internal callers/tests.
   202→_phase_unused = phase_unused
   203→_phase_structural = phase_structural
   204→_phase_coupling = phase_coupling
   205→_phase_responsibility_cohesion = phase_responsibility_cohesion
   206→_phase_uncalled_functions = phase_uncalled_functions
   207→_phase_smells = phase_smells
   208→_phase_mutable_state = phase_mutable_state
   209→_phase_layer_violation = phase_layer_violation
   210→_phase_dict_keys = phase_dict_keys
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "PY_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS",
   215→    "PY_ENTRY_PATTERNS",
   216→    "PY_GOD_RULES",
   217→    "PY_SKIP_NAMES",
   218→    "phase_coupling",
   219→    "phase_dict_keys",
   220→    "phase_layer_violation",
   221→    "phase_mutable_state",
   222→    "phase_responsibility_cohesion",
   223→    "phase_smells",
   224→    "phase_structural",
   225→    "phase_uncalled_functions",
   226→    "phase_unused",
   227→]
   228→
```

```
    25→def phase_smells(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    26→    """Run file/code smell detectors plus cross-file signature variance."""
    27→    entries, total_files = smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)
    28→    # Supplement with ruff B/E/W rules not covered by the regex smells above.
    29→    ruff_entries = detect_with_ruff_smells(path)
    30→    if ruff_entries:
    31→        entries = entries + ruff_entries
    32→    results = make_smell_findings(entries, log)
    33→
    34→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path) if lang.extract_functions else []
    35→    sig_entries, _ = signature_detector_mod.detect_signature_variance(functions)
    36→    for entry in sig_entries:
    37→        results.append(
    38→            state_mod.make_finding(
    39→                "smells",
    40→                entry["files"][0],
    41→                f"sig_variance::{entry['name']}",
    42→                tier=3,
    43→                confidence="medium",
    44→                summary=(
    45→                    f"Signature variance: {entry['name']}() has {entry['signature_count']} "
    46→                    f"different signatures across {entry['file_count']} files"
    47→                ),
    48→                detail={
    49→                    "function": entry["name"],
    50→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
    51→                    "signature_count": entry["signature_count"],
    52→                    "variants": entry["variants"][:5],
    53→                },
    54→            )
    55→        )
    56→    if sig_entries:
    57→        log(
    58→            f"         signature variance: {len(sig_entries)} functions with inconsistent signatures"
    59→        )
    60→
    61→    return results, {
    62→        "smells": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
    63→    }
    64→
    65→
    66→def phase_mutable_state(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    67→    """Find global mutable config patterns."""
    68→    entries, total_files = mutable_state_detector_mod.detect_global_mutable_config(path)
    69→    results = []
    70→    for entry in entries:
    71→        results.append(
    72→            state_mod.make_finding(
    73→                "global_mutable_config",
    74→                entry["file"],
    75→                entry["name"],
    76→                tier=3,
    77→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
    78→                summary=entry["summary"],
    79→                detail={
    80→                    "mutation_count": entry["mutation_count"],
    81→                    "mutation_lines": entry["mutation_lines"],
    82→                },
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→    if results:
    86→        log(f"         global mutable config: {len(results)} findings")
    87→    return results, {
    88→        "global_mutable_config": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
    89→    }

    92→def phase_layer_violation(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    93→    """Find package/layer boundary violations via import-linter."""
    94→    entries = import_linter_adapter_mod.detect_with_import_linter(path)
    95→    if entries is None:
    96→        return [], {}
    97→
    98→    total_files = len(lang.file_finder(path)) if lang.file_finder else 0
    99→    results = []
   100→    for entry in entries:
   101→        results.append(
   102→            state_mod.make_finding(
   103→                "layer_violation",
   104→                entry["file"],
   105→                f"{entry['source_pkg']}::{entry['target_pkg']}",
   106→                tier=2,
   107→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
   108→                summary=entry["summary"],
   109→                detail={
   110→                    "source_pkg": entry["source_pkg"],
   111→                    "target_pkg": entry["target_pkg"],
   112→                    "line": entry.get("line", 0),
   113→                    "description": entry.get("summary", ""),
   114→                },
   115→            )
   116→        )
   117→    if results:
   118→        log(f"         layer violations: {len(results)} findings")
   119→    return results, {"layer_violation": total_files}
   120→
   121→
   122→def phase_dict_keys(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   123→    """Run dict-key flow and schema-drift analysis."""
   124→    flow_entries, files_checked = dict_keys_detector_mod.detect_dict_key_flow(path)
   125→    flow_entries = filter_entries(lang.zone_map, flow_entries, "dict_keys")

   136→                tier=entry["tier"],

   145→            )

   148→    drift_entries, _ = dict_keys_detector_mod.detect_schema_drift(path)
   149→    drift_entries = filter_entries(lang.zone_map, drift_entries, "dict_keys")
   150→    for entry in drift_entries:
   151→        results.append(
   152→            state_mod.make_finding(
   153→                "dict_keys",
   154→                entry["file"],
   155→                f"schema_drift::{entry['key']}::{entry['line']}",
   156→                tier=entry["tier"],
   157→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
   158→                summary=entry["summary"],
   159→                detail={
   160→                    "kind": "schema_drift",
   161→                    "key": entry["key"],
   162→                    "line": entry["line"],
   163→                    "info": entry.get("detail", ""),
   164→                },
   165→            )
   166→        )
   167→
   168→    log(f"         -> {len(results)} dict key findings")
   169→    return results, {
```

```
1→"""Extracted heavy phase implementations for Python language pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.engine.detectors import complexity as complexity_detector_mod
     9→from desloppify.engine.detectors import flat_dirs as flat_dirs_detector_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.detectors import gods as gods_detector_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.detectors import graph as graph_detector_mod
    12→from desloppify.engine.detectors import large as large_detector_mod
    13→from desloppify.engine.detectors import orphaned as orphaned_detector_mod
    14→from desloppify.engine.detectors import single_use as single_use_detector_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import ComplexitySignal, GodRule
    16→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import adjust_potential, filter_entries
    17→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.structural import (
    18→    add_structural_signal,
    19→    merge_structural_signals,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.languages._framework.finding_factories import (
    22→    make_cycle_findings,
    23→    make_facade_findings,
    24→    make_orphaned_findings,
    25→    make_passthrough_findings,
    26→    make_single_use_findings,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun
    29→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors import (
    30→    coupling_contracts as coupling_contracts_detector_mod,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors import deps as deps_detector_mod
    33→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors import facade as facade_detector_mod
    34→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors import detect_passthrough_functions
    35→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors_classes import extract_py_classes
    36→from desloppify.state import Finding
    37→
    38→
    39→def run_phase_structural(
    40→    path: Path,
    41→    lang: LangRun,
    42→    *,
    43→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
    44→    god_rules: list[GodRule],
    45→    log_fn,
    46→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    47→    """Merge large + complexity + god classes into structural findings."""
    48→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
    49→
    50→    large_entries, file_count = large_detector_mod.detect_large_files(
    51→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder, threshold=lang.large_threshold
    52→    )
    53→    for entry in large_entries:
    54→        add_structural_signal(
    55→            structural,
    56→            entry["file"],
    57→            f"large ({entry['loc']} LOC)",
    58→            {"loc": entry["loc"]},
    59→        )
    60→
    61→    complexity_entries, _ = complexity_detector_mod.detect_complexity(
    62→        path,
    63→        signals=complexity_signals,
    64→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
    65→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
    66→    )
    67→    for entry in complexity_entries:
    68→        add_structural_signal(
    69→            structural,
    70→            entry["file"],
    71→            f"complexity score {entry['score']}",
    72→            {"complexity_score": entry["score"], "complexity_signals": entry["signals"]},
    73→        )
    74→        lang.complexity_map[entry["file"]] = entry["score"]
    75→
    76→    god_entries, _ = gods_detector_mod.detect_gods(extract_py_classes(path), god_rules)
    77→    for entry in god_entries:
    78→        add_structural_signal(
    79→            structural,
    80→            entry["file"],
    81→            entry["signal_text"],
    82→            entry["detail"],
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log_fn)
    86→
    87→    flat_entries, dir_count = flat_dirs_detector_mod.detect_flat_dirs(
    88→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder
    89→    )
    90→    for entry in flat_entries:
    91→        child_dir_count = int(entry.get("child_dir_count", 0))
    92→        combined_score = int(entry.get("combined_score", entry.get("file_count", 0)))
    93→        results.append(
    94→            state_mod.make_finding(
    95→                "flat_dirs",
    96→                entry["directory"],
    97→                "",
    98→                tier=3,
    99→                confidence="medium",
   100→                summary=flat_dirs_detector_mod.format_flat_dir_summary(entry),
   101→                detail={
   102→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
   103→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   104→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   105→                    "kind": entry.get("kind", "overload"),
   106→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(entry.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   107→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(entry.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   108→                    "sparse_child_count": int(entry.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   109→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(entry.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   110→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   111→                        entry.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   112→                    ),
   113→                },
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    if flat_entries:
   117→        log_fn(
   118→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   119→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   120→        )
   121→
   122→    passthrough_entries = detect_passthrough_functions(path)
   123→    results.extend(
   124→        make_passthrough_findings(
   125→            passthrough_entries,
   126→            "function",
   127→            "total_params",
   128→            log_fn,
   129→        )
   130→    )
   131→
   132→    potentials = {
   133→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   134→        "flat_dirs": dir_count,
   135→        "props": len(passthrough_entries) if passthrough_entries else 0,
   136→    }
   137→    return results, potentials
   138→
   139→
   140→def run_phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun, *, log_fn) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   141→    """Run coupling-related detectors and return findings/potentials."""
   142→    graph = deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph(path)
   143→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   144→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   145→
   146→    single_entries, single_candidates = (
   147→        single_use_detector_mod.detect_single_use_abstractions(
   148→            path, graph, barrel_names=lang.barrel_names
   149→        )
   150→    )
   151→    single_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, single_entries, "single_use")
   152→    results = make_single_use_findings(
   153→        single_entries, lang.get_area, skip_dir_names={"commands"}, stderr_fn=log_fn
   154→    )
   155→
   156→    cycle_entries, _ = graph_detector_mod.detect_cycles(graph)
   157→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   158→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log_fn))
   159→
   160→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = orphaned_detector_mod.detect_orphaned_files(
   161→        path,
   162→        graph,
   163→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   164→        options=orphaned_detector_mod.OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   165→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   166→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   167→            dynamic_import_finder=deps_detector_mod.find_python_dynamic_imports,
   168→        ),
   169→    )
   170→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   171→    results.extend(make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log_fn))
   172→
   173→    facade_entries, _ = facade_detector_mod.detect_reexport_facades(graph)
   174→    facade_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, facade_entries, "facade")
   175→    results.extend(make_facade_findings(facade_entries, log_fn))
   176→
   177→    mixin_entries, coupling_candidates = (
   178→        coupling_contracts_detector_mod.detect_implicit_mixin_contracts(path)
   179→    )
   180→    mixin_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, mixin_entries, "coupling")
   181→    for entry in mixin_entries:
   182→        attr_preview = ", ".join(entry["required_attrs"][:4])
   183→        if len(entry["required_attrs"]) > 4:
   184→            attr_preview += f", +{len(entry['required_attrs']) - 4} more"
   185→        required_count = entry["required_count"]
   186→        tier = 4 if required_count >= 6 else 3
   187→        confidence = "high" if required_count >= 6 else "medium"
   188→        results.append(
   189→            state_mod.make_finding(
   190→                "coupling",
   191→                entry["file"],
   192→                entry["class"],
   193→                tier=tier,
   194→                confidence=confidence,
   195→                summary=(
   196→                    f"Implicit host contract: {entry['class']} depends on {required_count} "
   197→                    f"undeclared self attrs ({attr_preview})"
   198→                ),
   199→                detail={
   200→                    "subtype": "implicit_mixin_contract",
   201→                    "required_attrs": entry["required_attrs"],
   202→                    "required_count": required_count,
   203→                    "line": entry.get("line"),
   204→                },
   205→            )
   206→        )
   207→
   208→    log_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   209→    potentials = {
   210→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zone_map, single_candidates),
   211→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   212→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   213→        "facade": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   214→        "coupling": adjust_potential(zone_map, coupling_candidates),
   215→    }
   216→    return results, potentials
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = ["run_phase_coupling", "run_phase_structural"]
   220→
```
