1→"""C# detector phase runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import ComplexitySignal, GodRule
     9→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.shared_phases import (
    11→    run_coupling_phase,
    12→    run_structural_phase,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRuntimeContract
    15→from desloppify.languages.csharp.detectors.deps import build_dep_graph
    16→from desloppify.languages.csharp.extractors import extract_csharp_classes
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import log
    18→
    19→
    20→def _compute_max_nesting(content: str, _lines: list[str]):
    21→    depth = 0
    22→    max_depth = 0
    23→    for ch in content:
    24→        if ch == "{":
    25→            depth += 1
    26→            max_depth = max(max_depth, depth)
    27→        elif ch == "}":
    28→            depth = max(0, depth - 1)
    29→    if max_depth > 8:
    30→        return max_depth, f"nesting depth {max_depth}"
    31→    return None
    32→
    33→
    34→def _compute_method_count(content: str, _lines: list[str]):
    35→    count = len(
    36→        re.findall(
    37→            r"(?m)^[ \t]*(?:(?:public|private|protected|internal|static|virtual|override|abstract|"
    38→            r"sealed|partial|async|extern|unsafe|new|required)\s+)+"
    39→            r"(?:[\w<>\[\],\.\?]+\s+)+[A-Za-z_]\w*\s*\(",
    40→            content,
    41→        )
    42→    )
    43→    if count > 20:
    44→        return count, f"{count} methods"
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def _compute_long_methods(content: str, _lines: list[str]):
    49→    # Heuristic: count methods with >= 60 logical lines.
    50→    blocks = re.findall(
    51→        r"(?ms)^[ \t]*(?:(?:public|private|protected|internal|static|virtual|override|abstract|"
    52→        r"sealed|partial|async|extern|unsafe|new|required)\s+)+"
    53→        r"(?:[\w<>\[\],\.\?]+\s+)+[A-Za-z_]\w*\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{(.*?)^\s*\}",
    54→        content,
    55→    )
    56→    long_count = 0
    57→    for body in blocks:
    58→        logical = [
    59→            ln
    60→            for ln in body.splitlines()
    61→            if ln.strip() and not ln.strip().startswith("//")
    62→        ]
    63→        if len(logical) >= 60:
    64→            long_count += 1
    65→    if long_count > 0:
    66→        return long_count, f"{long_count} long methods"
    67→    return None
    68→
    69→
    70→CSHARP_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS = [
    71→    ComplexitySignal(
    72→        "imports", r"(?m)^\s*(?:global\s+)?using\s+", weight=1, threshold=20
    73→    ),
    74→    ComplexitySignal(
    75→        "TODOs", r"(?m)//\s*(?:TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX)", weight=2, threshold=0
    76→    ),
    77→    ComplexitySignal(
    78→        "many classes",
    79→        r"(?m)^\s*(?:public\s+)?(?:class|record|struct)\s+\w+",
    80→        weight=2,
    81→        threshold=5,
    82→    ),
    83→    ComplexitySignal(
    84→        "deep nesting", None, weight=2, threshold=0, compute=_compute_max_nesting
    85→    ),
    86→    ComplexitySignal(
    87→        "many methods", None, weight=2, threshold=0, compute=_compute_method_count
    88→    ),
    89→    ComplexitySignal(
    90→        "long methods", None, weight=2, threshold=0, compute=_compute_long_methods
    91→    ),
    92→]
    93→
    94→CSHARP_GOD_RULES = [
    95→    GodRule("methods", "methods", lambda c: len(c.methods), 15),
    96→    GodRule("attributes", "attributes", lambda c: len(c.attributes), 10),
    97→    GodRule(
    98→        "base_classes", "base classes/interfaces", lambda c: len(c.base_classes), 4
    99→    ),
   100→    GodRule(
   101→        "long_methods",
   102→        "long methods (>50 LOC)",
   103→        lambda c: sum(1 for m in c.methods if m.loc > 50),
   104→        2,
   105→    ),
   106→]
   107→
   108→
   109→def _runtime_setting(lang: LangRuntimeContract, key: str, default: int) -> int:
   110→    """Read language setting from runtime context."""
   111→    try:
   112→        return int(lang.runtime_setting(key, default))
   113→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   114→        return default
   115→
   116→
   117→def _corroboration_signals_for_csharp(
   118→    entry: dict, lang: LangRuntimeContract
   119→) -> tuple[list[str], int, int]:
   120→    """Return corroboration signals plus complexity/import counts for confidence gating."""
   121→    filepath = entry.get("file", "")
   122→    loc = entry.get("loc", 0)
   123→    import_count = entry.get("import_count", 0)
   124→    fanout_threshold = max(1, _runtime_setting(lang, "high_fanout_threshold", 5))
   125→    complexity_map = lang.complexity_map
   126→    complexity_score = complexity_map.get(filepath, 0)
   127→    if complexity_score == 0 and filepath:
   128→        complexity_score = complexity_map.get(rel(filepath), 0)
   129→
   130→    signals: list[str] = []
   131→    if loc >= lang.large_threshold:
   132→        signals.append(f"large ({loc} LOC)")
   133→    if complexity_score >= lang.complexity_threshold:
   134→        signals.append(f"complexity ({complexity_score})")
   135→    if import_count >= fanout_threshold:
   136→        signals.append(f"high fan-out ({import_count} imports)")
   137→    return signals, complexity_score, import_count
   138→
   139→
   140→def _apply_csharp_actionability_gates(
   141→    findings: list[dict], entries: list[dict], lang: LangRuntimeContract
   142→) -> None:
   143→    """Downgrade actionability unless multiple independent signals corroborate."""
   144→    min_signals = max(1, _runtime_setting(lang, "corroboration_min_signals", 2))
   145→    entries_by_file = {rel(e["file"]): e for e in entries}
   146→    for finding in findings:
   147→        entry = entries_by_file.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   148→        if not entry:
   149→            continue
   150→        signals, complexity_score, import_count = _corroboration_signals_for_csharp(
   151→            entry, lang
   152→        )
   153→        corroboration_count = len(signals)
   154→        finding["confidence"] = (
   155→            "medium" if corroboration_count >= min_signals else "low"
   156→        )
   157→        detail = finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   158→        detail["corroboration_signals"] = signals
   159→        detail["corroboration_count"] = corroboration_count
   160→        detail["corroboration_min_required"] = min_signals
   161→        detail["complexity_score"] = complexity_score
   162→        detail["import_count"] = import_count
   163→
   164→
   165→def _phase_structural(
   166→    path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract
   167→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   168→    """Merge large + complexity + god classes into structural findings."""
   169→    return run_structural_phase(
   170→        path,
   171→        lang,
   172→        complexity_signals=CSHARP_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS,
   173→        log_fn=log,
   174→        god_rules=CSHARP_GOD_RULES,
   175→        god_extractor_fn=extract_csharp_classes,
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→def _phase_coupling(
   180→    path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract
   181→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   182→    """Run coupling-oriented detectors on the C# dependency graph."""
   183→    roslyn_cmd = str(lang.runtime_option("roslyn_cmd", "") or "")
   184→
   185→    def _build(p):
   186→        return build_dep_graph(p, roslyn_cmd=(roslyn_cmd or None))
   187→
   188→    return run_coupling_phase(
   189→        path,
   190→        lang,
   191→        build_dep_graph_fn=_build,
   192→        log_fn=log,
   193→        post_process_fn=_apply_csharp_actionability_gates,
   194→    )
   195→
```

1→"""Tree-sitter phase factories for language plugins.
     2→
     3→Each factory takes a ``TreeSitterLangSpec`` and returns a ``DetectorPhase``.
     4→All phases resolve ``lang.file_finder`` at runtime.
     5→
     6→Used by both generic plugins (via ``generic.py``) and full plugins (C#, Dart,
     7→GDScript, TypeScript) that want tree-sitter-powered detection without
     8→duplicating the phase construction logic.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase
    14→from desloppify.state import make_finding
    15→from desloppify.core.output_api import log
    16→
    17→# ── Phase factories ────────────────────────────────────────
    18→
    19→
    20→def make_ast_smells_phase(spec) -> DetectorPhase:
    21→    """Create an AST smells phase: empty catches + unreachable code."""
    22→
    23→    def run(path, lang):
    24→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._smells import (
    25→            detect_empty_catches,
    26→            detect_unreachable_code,
    27→        )
    28→
    29→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
    30→        findings = []
    31→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    32→
    33→        catches = detect_empty_catches(file_list, spec)
    34→        for e in catches:
    35→            findings.append(make_finding(
    36→                "smells", e["file"], f"empty_catch::{e['line']}",
    37→                tier=3, confidence="high",
    38→                summary=f"Empty {e['type']} — swallows errors silently",
    39→            ))
    40→        if catches:
    41→            potentials["empty_catch"] = len(catches)
    42→            log(f"         empty catch blocks: {len(catches)}")
    43→
    44→        unreachable = detect_unreachable_code(file_list, spec)
    45→        for e in unreachable:
    46→            findings.append(make_finding(
    47→                "smells", e["file"], f"unreachable_code::{e['line']}",
    48→                tier=3, confidence="high",
    49→                summary=f"Unreachable code after {e['after']}",
    50→            ))
    51→        if unreachable:
    52→            potentials["unreachable_code"] = len(unreachable)
    53→            log(f"         unreachable code: {len(unreachable)}")
    54→
    55→        return findings, potentials
    56→
    57→    return DetectorPhase("AST smells", run)
    58→
    59→
    60→def make_cohesion_phase(spec) -> DetectorPhase:
    61→    """Create a responsibility cohesion phase."""
    62→
    63→    def run(path, lang):
    64→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._cohesion import (
    65→            detect_responsibility_cohesion,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
    69→        findings = []
    70→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    71→
    72→        entries, checked = detect_responsibility_cohesion(file_list, spec)
    73→        for e in entries:
    74→            families = ", ".join(e["families"][:4])
    75→            findings.append(make_finding(
    76→                "responsibility_cohesion", e["file"],
    77→                f"cohesion::{e['file']}",
    78→                tier=3, confidence="medium",
    79→                summary=(
    80→                    f"{e['component_count']} disconnected function clusters "
    81→                    f"({e['function_count']} functions) — likely mixed responsibilities"
    82→                ),
    83→                detail=f"Clusters: {families}",
    84→            ))
    85→        if entries:
    86→            potentials["responsibility_cohesion"] = len(entries)
    87→            log(f"         low-cohesion files: {len(entries)}")
    88→
    89→        return findings, potentials
    90→
    91→    return DetectorPhase("Responsibility cohesion", run)
    92→
    93→
    94→def make_unused_imports_phase(spec) -> DetectorPhase:
    95→    """Create an unused imports phase."""
    96→
    97→    def run(path, lang):
    98→        from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter._unused_imports import (
    99→            detect_unused_imports,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→        file_list = lang.file_finder(path)
   103→        findings = []
   104→        potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
   105→
   106→        entries = detect_unused_imports(file_list, spec)
   107→        for e in entries:
   108→            findings.append(make_finding(
   109→                "unused", e["file"], f"unused_import::{e['line']}",
   110→                tier=3, confidence="medium",
   111→                summary=f"Unused import: {e['name']}",
   112→            ))
   113→        if entries:
   114→            potentials["unused_imports"] = len(entries)
   115→            log(f"         unused imports: {len(entries)}")
   116→
   117→        return findings, potentials
   118→
   119→    return DetectorPhase("Unused imports", run)
   120→
   121→
   122→# ── Convenience: all tree-sitter phases for a named language ──
   123→
   124→
   125→def all_treesitter_phases(spec_name: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
   126→    """Return all tree-sitter-powered phases for a language plugin.
   127→
   128→    Convenience bundle — returns AST smells, cohesion, and (when import
   129→    query exists) unused imports.  Returns [] if tree-sitter-language-pack
   130→    is not installed.
   131→    """
   132→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import get_spec, is_available
   133→
   134→    if not is_available():
   135→        return []
   136→
   137→    spec = get_spec(spec_name)
   138→    if spec is None or not spec.function_query:
   139→        return []
   140→
   141→    phases = [
   142→        make_ast_smells_phase(spec),
   143→        make_cohesion_phase(spec),
   144→    ]
   145→
   146→    if spec.import_query:
   147→        phases.append(make_unused_imports_phase(spec))
   148→
   149→    return phases
   150→
   151→
   152→__all__ = [
   153→    "all_treesitter_phases",
   154→    "make_ast_smells_phase",
   155→    "make_cohesion_phase",
   156→    "make_unused_imports_phase",
   157→]
   158→
```

1→"""DetectorPhase builder helpers for shared framework phases."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .shared_phases import (
     6→    phase_boilerplate_duplication,
     7→    phase_dupes,
     8→    phase_security,
     9→    phase_signature,
    10→    phase_subjective_review,
    11→    phase_test_coverage,
    12→)
    13→from .types import DetectorPhase
    14→
    15→
    16→def _phase_factory(label: str, run_fn, *, slow: bool = False):
    17→    """Return a phase factory with explicit label/run/slow semantics."""
    18→    def factory() -> DetectorPhase:
    19→        return DetectorPhase(label, run_fn, slow=slow)
    20→    return factory
    21→
    22→
    23→SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES = {
    24→    "test_coverage": _phase_factory("Test coverage", phase_test_coverage),
    25→    "security": _phase_factory("Security", phase_security),
    26→    "signature": _phase_factory("Signature analysis", phase_signature),
    27→    "subjective_review": _phase_factory("Subjective review", phase_subjective_review),
    28→    "duplicates": _phase_factory("Duplicates", phase_dupes, slow=True),
    29→    "boilerplate_duplication": _phase_factory(
    30→        "Boilerplate duplication",
    31→        phase_boilerplate_duplication,
    32→        slow=True,
    33→    ),
    34→}
    35→
    36→
    37→detector_phase_test_coverage = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["test_coverage"]
    38→detector_phase_security = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["security"]
    39→detector_phase_signature = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["signature"]
    40→detector_phase_subjective_review = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["subjective_review"]
    41→detector_phase_duplicates = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["duplicates"]
    42→detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["boilerplate_duplication"]
    43→
    44→
    45→def shared_subjective_duplicates_tail(
    46→    *,
    47→    pre_duplicates: list[DetectorPhase] | None = None,
    48→) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    49→    """Shared review tail: subjective review, optional custom phases, then duplicates."""
    50→    phases = [detector_phase_subjective_review()]
    51→    if pre_duplicates:
    52→        phases.extend(pre_duplicates)
    53→    phases.append(detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication())
    54→    phases.append(detector_phase_duplicates())
    55→    return phases
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = [
    59→    "detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication",
    60→    "detector_phase_duplicates",
    61→    "detector_phase_security",
    62→    "detector_phase_signature",
    63→    "detector_phase_subjective_review",
    64→    "detector_phase_test_coverage",
    65→    "SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES",
    66→    "shared_subjective_duplicates_tail",
    67→]
    68→

    71→def _ts_treesitter_phases() -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    72→    """Cherry-pick tree-sitter phases that complement TS's own detectors.
    73→
    74→    TS already has its own smells and unused detection — only add cohesion
    75→    which TS lacks.  (Signature analysis is added separately since it's
    76→    backend-agnostic and doesn't require tree-sitter.)
    77→    """
    78→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import get_spec, is_available
    79→
    80→    if not is_available():
    81→        return []
    82→
    83→    spec = get_spec("typescript")
    84→    if spec is None:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    return [
    88→        make_cohesion_phase(spec),
    89→    ]
    90→
    91→
    92→_TS_TEST_COVERAGE_HOOKS = (
    93→    ts_test_coverage_hooks.has_testable_logic,
    94→    ts_test_coverage_hooks.resolve_import_spec,
    95→    ts_test_coverage_hooks.map_test_to_source,
    96→)
    97→
    98→
    99→register_lang_hooks("typescript", test_coverage=ts_test_coverage_hooks)
   100→

   127→
   128→def _det_unused(cat):
   129→    """Create a detector function for a specific unused category."""
   130→    def f(path):
   131→        return unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path, category=cat)[0]
   132→    return f
   133→
   134→
   135→def _det_logs(path):
   136→    """Detect tagged debug logs."""
   137→    return logs_detector_mod.detect_logs(path)[0]
   138→
   139→
   140→def _det_smell(smell_id):
   141→    """Create a detector function for a specific smell ID."""
   142→    def f(path):
   143→        return next(
   144→            (
   145→                e.get("matches", [])
   146→                for e in smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)[0]
   147→                if e["id"] == smell_id
   148→            ),
   149→            [],
   150→        )
   151→    return f
   152→
   153→
   154→def _fix_vars(entries, *, dry_run=False):
   155→    """Fix unused vars, returning FixResult."""
   156→    results, skip_reasons = ts_fixers_mod.fix_unused_vars(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
   157→    return FixResult(entries=results, skip_reasons=skip_reasons)
   158→
   159→
   160→def _fix_logs(entries, *, dry_run=False):
   161→    """Fix debug logs, normalizing result keys."""
   162→    results = ts_fixers_mod.fix_debug_logs(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
   163→    for r in results:
   164→        r["removed"] = r.get("tags", r.get("removed", []))
   165→    return results
   166→
   167→
   168→def _get_ts_fixers() -> dict[str, FixerConfig]:
   169→    """Build the TypeScript fixer registry (lazy-loaded).
   170→
   171→    Detection and fix functions use lazy imports so detector modules
   172→    aren't loaded until the fix command actually runs.
   173→    """
   174→    R, DV = "Removed", "Would remove"
   175→    return {
   176→        "unused-imports": FixerConfig(
   177→            "unused imports",
   178→            _det_unused("imports"),
   179→            ts_fixers_mod.fix_unused_imports,
   180→            "unused",
   181→            R,
   182→            DV,
   183→        ),
   184→        "debug-logs": FixerConfig(
   185→            "tagged debug logs", _det_logs, _fix_logs, "logs", R, DV
   186→        ),
   187→        "unused-vars": FixerConfig(
   188→            "unused vars", _det_unused("vars"), _fix_vars, "unused", R, DV
   189→        ),
   190→        "unused-params": FixerConfig(
   191→            "unused params",
   192→            _det_unused("vars"),
   193→            ts_fixers_mod.fix_unused_params,
   194→            "unused",
   195→            "Prefixed",
   196→            "Would prefix",
   197→        ),
   198→        "dead-useeffect": FixerConfig(
   199→            "dead useEffect calls",
   200→            _det_smell("dead_useeffect"),
   201→            ts_fixers_mod.fix_dead_useeffect,
   202→            "smells",
   203→            R,
   204→            DV,
   205→        ),
   206→        "empty-if-chain": FixerConfig(
   207→            "empty if/else chains",
   208→            _det_smell("empty_if_chain"),
   209→            ts_fixers_mod.fix_empty_if_chain,
   210→            "smells",
   211→            R,
   212→            DV,
   213→        ),
   214→    }
   215→
   216→
   217→# ── Build the config ──────────────────────────────────────
   218→
   219→
   220→
   221→def _ts_extract_functions(path: Path) -> list[FunctionInfo]:
   222→    """Extract all TS functions for duplicate detection."""
   223→    functions = []
   224→    for filepath in find_ts_files(path):
   225→        if "node_modules" in filepath or ".d.ts" in filepath:
   226→            continue
   227→        functions.extend(extract_ts_functions(filepath))
   228→    return functions
   229→
   230→
   231→@register_lang("typescript")
   232→class TypeScriptConfig(LangConfig):
   233→    def detect_lang_security_detailed(self, files, zone_map):
   234→        result = detect_ts_security_result(files, zone_map)
   235→        return LangSecurityResult(
   236→            entries=result.entries,
   237→            files_scanned=result.population_size,
   238→        )
   239→
   240→    def __init__(self):
   241→        super().__init__(
   242→            name="typescript",
   243→            extensions=[".ts", ".tsx"],
   244→            exclusions=["node_modules", ".d.ts"],
   245→            default_src="src",
   246→            build_dep_graph=deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph,
   247→            entry_patterns=[
   248→                "/pages/",
   249→                "/main.tsx",
   250→                "/main.ts",
   251→                "/App.tsx",
   252→                "vite.config",
   253→                "tailwind.config",
   254→                "postcss.config",
   255→                ".d.ts",
   256→                "/settings.ts",
   257→                "/__tests__/",
   258→                ".test.",
   259→                ".spec.",
   260→                ".stories.",
   261→            ],
   262→            barrel_names={"index.ts", "index.tsx"},
   263→            phases=[
   264→                DetectorPhase("Logs", phase_logs),
   265→                DetectorPhase("Unused (tsc)", phase_unused),
   266→                DetectorPhase("Dead exports", phase_exports),
   267→                DetectorPhase("Deprecated", phase_deprecated),
   268→                DetectorPhase("Structural analysis", phase_structural),
   269→                DetectorPhase(
   270→                    "Coupling + single-use + patterns + naming", phase_coupling
   271→                ),
   272→                *_ts_treesitter_phases(),
   273→                detector_phase_signature(),
   274→                detector_phase_test_coverage(),
   275→                DetectorPhase("Code smells", phase_smells),
   276→                detector_phase_security(),
   277→                *shared_subjective_duplicates_tail(),
   278→            ],
   279→            fixers=_get_ts_fixers(),
   280→            get_area=get_area,
   281→            detect_commands=ts_commands_mod.get_detect_commands(),
   282→            boundaries=[

100→
   101→def _py_extract_functions(path: Path) -> list[FunctionInfo]:
   102→    """Extract all Python functions for duplicate detection."""
   103→    functions = []
   104→    for filepath in find_py_files(path):
   105→        functions.extend(extract_py_functions(filepath))
   106→    return functions
   107→
   108→
   109→def _scan_root_from_files(files: list[str]) -> Path | None:
   110→    """Derive the common ancestor directory from a list of file paths."""
   111→    import os
   112→
   113→    py_files = [f for f in files if f.endswith(".py")]
   114→    if not py_files:
   115→        return None
   116→    try:
   117→        common = Path(os.path.commonpath(py_files))
   118→        return common if common.is_dir() else common.parent
   119→    except ValueError:
   120→        return None
   121→
   122→
   123→@register_lang("python")
   124→class PythonConfig(LangConfig):
   125→    def _missing_bandit_coverage(self) -> DetectorCoverageStatus:
   126→        return DetectorCoverageStatus(
   127→            detector="security",
   128→            status="reduced",
   129→            confidence=0.6,
   130→            summary="bandit is not installed — Python-specific security checks will be skipped.",
   131→            impact=(
   132→                "Python-specific security coverage is reduced; clean security output may "
   133→                "miss shell injection, unsafe deserialization, and risky SQL/subprocess patterns."
   134→            ),
   135→            remediation="Install Bandit: pip install bandit",
   136→            tool="bandit",
   137→            reason="missing_dependency",
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    def scan_coverage_prerequisites(self) -> list[DetectorCoverageStatus]:
   141→        if shutil.which("bandit") is not None:
   142→            return []
   143→        return [self._missing_bandit_coverage()]
   144→
   145→    def detect_lang_security_detailed(self, files, zone_map) -> LangSecurityResult:
   146→        scan_root = _scan_root_from_files(files)
   147→        if scan_root is None:
   148→            return LangSecurityResult(entries=[], files_scanned=0)
   149→
   150→        exclude_dirs = collect_exclude_dirs(scan_root)
   151→
   152→        result = detect_with_bandit(scan_root, zone_map, exclude_dirs=exclude_dirs)
   153→        coverage = result.status.coverage()
   154→        return LangSecurityResult(
   155→            entries=result.entries,
   156→            files_scanned=result.files_scanned,
   157→            coverage=coverage,
   158→        )
   159→
   160→    def detect_private_imports(self, graph, zone_map):
   161→        return detect_python_private_imports(graph, zone_map)
   162→
   163→    def __init__(self):
   164→        super().__init__(

## Investigation Summary: Detector Multi-Phase Call Instances

Based on my thorough investigation of the desloppify codebase, I've identified **ALL instances** where detectors are called from multiple phases, causing duplicate findings with different ID namespaces.

### Known Instance (Already Identified)

**1. `detect_signature_variance()`** — CONFIRMED DUPLICATE BUG

- **Shared phase**: `phase_signature()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py:418-450`
  - Creates findings with ID: `signature_variance::{name}`
  - Detector namespace: `"signature"`

- **TypeScript phase**: `phase_smells()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py:582-695` (lines 586-610)
  - Calls `detect_signature_variance()` again
  - Creates findings with ID: `sig_variance::{name}`
  - Detector namespace: `"smells"`

- **Python phase**: `phase_smells()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/phases_quality.py:25-63` (lines 34-59)
  - Calls `detect_signature_variance()` again
  - Creates findings with ID: `sig_variance::{name}`
  - Detector namespace: `"smells"`

**Phase Registration Pattern** (showing how both phases execute for each language):

- **TypeScript** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/__init__.py:263-278`):
  - Line 273: `detector_phase_signature()` — runs shared phase
  - Line 275: `DetectorPhase("Code smells", phase_smells)` — runs language-specific phase with duplicate detection

- **Python** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/__init__.py:172-186`):
  - Line 179: `DetectorPhase("Code smells", phase_smells)` — runs language-specific phase that ALSO calls signature variance
  - Note: Python does NOT have `detector_phase_signature()` in its phase list, so the duplication is asymmetric

- **C#** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/csharp/__init__.py:115-123`):
  - Line 119: `detector_phase_signature()` — runs shared phase
  - No language-specific smells phase, so NO duplication here

- **Go** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/go/__init__.py:63-80`):
  - Line 76: `detector_phase_signature()` — runs shared phase only
  - No language-specific smells phase, so NO duplication

- **Dart** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/dart/__init__.py:76-84`):
  - Line 80: `detector_phase_signature()` — runs shared phase only
  - No language-specific smells phase, so NO duplication

- **GDScript** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/gdscript/__init__.py:69-77`):
  - Line 73: `detector_phase_signature()` — runs shared phase only
  - No language-specific smells phase, so NO duplication

### Summary of All Detectors Used in Shared Phases

The following shared detectors are called from shared phases only (NO duplication):

1. **`detect_dupes()`** — called from `phase_dupes()` in shared_phases.py
2. **`detect_complexity()`** — called from `run_structural_phase()` in shared_phases.py
3. **`detect_cycles()`** — called from `run_coupling_phase()` in shared_phases.py
4. **`detect_large_files()`** — called from `run_structural_phase()` in shared_phases.py
5. **`detect_flat_dirs()`** — called from `run_structural_phase()` in shared_phases.py
6. **`detect_gods()`** — called from `run_structural_phase()` in shared_phases.py
7. **`detect_single_use_abstractions()`** — called from `run_coupling_phase()` in shared_phases.py
8. **`detect_orphaned_files()`** — called from `run_coupling_phase()` in shared_phases.py
9. **`detect_security_issues()`** — called from `phase_security()` in shared_phases.py
10. **`detect_test_coverage()`** — called from `phase_test_coverage()` in shared_phases.py

### Files Involved in Duplication

- **Shared detector**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/detectors/signature.py:59-147`
- **Shared phase**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py:418-450`
- **TypeScript smells phase**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py:582-610`
- **Python smells phase**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/phases_quality.py:34-59`

### Root Cause Analysis

The bug pattern is:
1. A shared detector function (`detect_signature_variance()`) is designed to be reusable
2. It's wrapped in a shared phase (`phase_signature()`) that creates findings under the `"signature"` namespace
3. TypeScript and Python language-specific phases independently decided to call the SAME detector in their own `phase_smells()` implementation
4. This creates duplicate findings under DIFFERENT namespaces (`"smells"` instead of `"signature"`), with slightly different finding IDs (`sig_variance::` vs `signature_variance::`)

### Conclusion

**There is ONLY ONE instance of the multi-phase detector duplication bug:** `detect_signature_variance()` being called from both the shared `phase_signature()` AND from language-specific `phase_smells()` phases (TypeScript and Python only).

No other shared detectors exhibit this pattern. All other shared detectors are called from a single phase only.

    54→
    55→
    56→def phase_dupes(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    57→    """Shared phase runner: detect duplicate functions via lang.extract_functions.
    58→
    59→    When a zone map is available, filters out functions from zone-excluded files
    60→    before the O(n^2) comparison to avoid test/config/generated false positives.
    61→    """
    62→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path)
    63→
    64→    # Filter out functions from zone-excluded files.
    65→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
    66→        before = len(functions)
    67→        functions = [
    68→            f
    69→            for f in functions
    70→            if lang.zone_map.get(getattr(f, "file", "")) not in EXCLUDED_ZONES
    71→        ]
    72→        excluded = before - len(functions)
    73→        if excluded:
    74→            log(f"         zones: {excluded} functions excluded (non-production)")
    75→
    76→    entries, total_functions = detect_duplicates(functions)
    77→    findings = make_dupe_findings(entries, log)
    78→    return findings, {"dupes": total_functions}
    79→
    80→
    81→def phase_boilerplate_duplication(
    82→    path: Path,
    83→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
    84→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    85→    """Shared phase runner: detect repeated boilerplate code via jscpd."""
    86→    entries = detect_with_jscpd(path)
    87→    if entries is None:
    88→        return [], {}
    89→    entries = _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone(entries, lang.zone_map)
    90→
    91→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    92→    for entry in entries:
    93→        locations = entry["locations"]
    94→        first = locations[0]
    95→        loc_preview = ", ".join(
    96→            f"{rel(item['file'])}:{item['line']}" for item in locations[:4]
    97→        )
    98→        if len(locations) > 4:
    99→            loc_preview += f", +{len(locations) - 4} more"
   100→        findings.append(
   101→            make_finding(
   102→                "boilerplate_duplication",
   103→                first["file"],
   104→                entry["id"],
   105→                tier=3,
   106→                confidence="medium",
   107→                summary=(
   108→                    f"Boilerplate block repeated across {entry['distinct_files']} files "
   109→                    f"(window {entry['window_size']} lines): {loc_preview}"
   110→                ),
   111→                detail={
   112→                    "distinct_files": entry["distinct_files"],
   113→                    "window_size": entry["window_size"],
   114→                    "locations": locations,
   115→                    "sample": entry["sample"],
   116→                },
   117→            )
   118→        )
   119→
   120→    if findings:
   121→        log(f"         boilerplate duplication: {len(findings)} clusters")
   122→    distinct_files = len({loc["file"] for e in entries for loc in e["locations"]})
   123→    return findings, {"boilerplate_duplication": distinct_files}
   124→
   125→
   126→def _filter_boilerplate_entries_by_zone(entries: list[dict], zone_map) -> list[dict]:
   127→    """Keep only in-scope, zone-allowed boilerplate clusters.
   128→
   129→    jscpd can return files that are outside language discovery (artifacts/docs).
   130→    Restricting to known zone-map files prevents unknown paths from being treated
   131→    as production findings.
   132→    """
   133→    if zone_map is None:
   134→        return entries
   135→
   136→    known_files = set(zone_map.all_files())
   137→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   138→    skipped = 0
   139→    for entry in entries:
   140→        locations = entry.get("locations", [])
   141→        kept_locations = [
   142→            loc
   143→            for loc in locations
   144→            if loc.get("file") in known_files
   145→            and not should_skip_finding(
   146→                zone_map,
   147→                loc.get("file", ""),
   148→                "boilerplate_duplication",
   149→            )
   150→        ]
   151→        distinct_files = {loc["file"] for loc in kept_locations}
   152→        if len(distinct_files) < 2:
   153→            skipped += 1
   154→            continue
   155→
   156→        normalized = dict(entry)
   157→        normalized["locations"] = sorted(
   158→            kept_locations,
   159→            key=lambda item: (item.get("file", ""), item.get("line", 0)),
   160→        )
   161→        normalized["distinct_files"] = len(distinct_files)
   162→        filtered.append(normalized)
   163→
   164→    if skipped:
   165→        log(f"         zones: {skipped} boilerplate clusters excluded")
   166→    return filtered
   167→
   168→
   169→def find_external_test_files(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> set[str]:
   170→    """Find test files in standard locations outside the scanned path."""
   171→    extra = set()
   172→    path_root = path.resolve()
   173→    project_root = get_project_root()
   174→    test_dirs = lang.external_test_dirs or ["tests", "test"]
   175→    exts = tuple(lang.test_file_extensions or lang.extensions)
   176→    for test_dir in test_dirs:
   177→        d = project_root / test_dir
   178→        if not d.is_dir():
   179→            continue
   180→        if d.resolve().is_relative_to(path_root):
   181→            continue  # test_dir is inside scanned path, zone_map already has it
   182→        for root, _, files in os.walk(d):
   183→            for filename in files:
   184→                if any(filename.endswith(ext) for ext in exts):
   185→                    extra.add(os.path.join(root, filename))
   186→    return extra
   187→
   188→
   189→def _entries_to_findings(
   190→    detector: str,
   191→    entries: list[dict],
   192→    *,
   193→    default_name: str = "",
   194→    include_zone: bool = False,
   195→    zone_map=None,
   196→) -> list[Finding]:
   197→    """Convert detector entries to normalized findings."""
   198→    results: list[Finding] = []
   199→    for entry in entries:
   200→        finding = make_finding(
   201→            detector,
   202→            entry["file"],
   203→            entry.get("name", default_name),
   204→            tier=entry["tier"],
   205→            confidence=entry["confidence"],
   206→            summary=entry["summary"],
   207→            detail=entry.get("detail", {}),
   208→        )
   209→        if include_zone and zone_map is not None:
   210→            z = zone_map.get(entry["file"])
   211→            if z is not None:
   212→                finding["zone"] = z.value
   213→        results.append(finding)
   214→    return results
   215→
   216→
   217→def _log_phase_summary(label: str, results: list[Finding], potential: int, unit: str) -> None:
   218→    """Emit standardized shared-phase summary logging."""
   219→    if results:
   220→        log(f"         {label}: {len(results)} findings ({potential} {unit})")
   221→    else:
   222→        log(f"         {label}: clean ({potential} {unit})")
   223→
   224→
   225→def _coverage_to_dict(coverage: DetectorCoverageStatus) -> dict[str, object]:
   226→    return {
   227→        "detector": coverage.detector,
   228→        "status": coverage.status,
   229→        "confidence": round(coerce_confidence(coverage.confidence), 2),
   230→        "summary": coverage.summary,
   231→        "impact": coverage.impact,
   232→        "remediation": coverage.remediation,
   233→        "tool": coverage.tool,
   234→        "reason": coverage.reason,
   235→    }
   236→
   237→
   238→def _merge_detector_coverage(
   239→    existing: dict[str, object],
   240→    incoming: dict[str, object],
   241→) -> dict[str, object]:
   242→    merged = dict(existing)
   243→    merged["status"] = (
   244→        "reduced"
   245→        if str(existing.get("status", "full")) == "reduced"
   246→        or str(incoming.get("status", "full")) == "reduced"
   247→        else "full"
   248→    )
   249→    merged["confidence"] = round(
   250→        min(
   251→            coerce_confidence(existing.get("confidence")),
   252→            coerce_confidence(incoming.get("confidence")),
   253→        ),
   254→        2,
   255→    )
   256→
   257→    for key in ("summary", "impact", "remediation", "tool", "reason"):
   258→        current = str(merged.get(key, "") or "").strip()
   259→        update = str(incoming.get(key, "") or "").strip()
   260→        if update and not current:
   261→            merged[key] = update
   262→        elif update and current and update not in current:
   263→            merged[key] = f"{current} | {update}"
   264→    return merged
   265→
   266→
   267→def _record_detector_coverage(lang: LangRuntimeContract, coverage: DetectorCoverageStatus | None) -> None:
   268→    if coverage is None:
   269→        return
   270→    normalized = _coverage_to_dict(coverage)
   271→    detector = str(normalized.get("detector", "")).strip()
   272→    if not detector:
   273→        return
   274→    existing = lang.detector_coverage.get(detector)
   275→    if isinstance(existing, dict):
   276→        lang.detector_coverage[detector] = _merge_detector_coverage(existing, normalized)
   277→    else:
   278→        lang.detector_coverage[detector] = normalized
   279→
   280→
   281→def phase_security(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   282→    """Shared phase: detect security issues (cross-language + lang-specific)."""
   283→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   284→    files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
   285→    entries, cross_lang_scanned = detect_security_issues(
   286→        files,
   287→        zone_map,
   288→        lang.name,
   289→        scan_root=path,
   290→    )
   291→    lang_scanned = 0
   292→
   293→    # Also call lang-specific security detectors.
   294→    lang_result = lang.detect_lang_security_detailed(files, zone_map)
   295→    lang_entries = lang_result.entries
   296→    lang_scanned = max(0, int(lang_result.files_scanned))
   297→    _record_detector_coverage(lang, lang_result.coverage)
   298→    entries.extend(lang_entries)
   299→
   300→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "security")
   301→    potential = max(cross_lang_scanned, lang_scanned)
   302→
   303→    results = _entries_to_findings(
   304→        "security",
   305→        entries,
   306→        include_zone=True,
   307→        zone_map=zone_map,
   308→    )
   309→    _log_phase_summary("security", results, potential, "files scanned")
   310→
   311→    if "security" not in lang.detector_coverage:
   312→        lang.detector_coverage["security"] = {
   313→            "detector": "security",
   314→            "status": "full",
   315→            "confidence": 1.0,
   316→            "summary": "Security coverage complete for enabled detectors.",
   317→            "impact": "",
   318→            "remediation": "",
   319→            "tool": "",
   320→            "reason": "",
   321→        }
   322→
   323→    return results, {"security": potential}
   324→
   325→
   326→def phase_test_coverage(
   327→    path: Path,
   328→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   329→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   330→    """Shared phase: detect test coverage gaps."""
   331→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   332→    if zone_map is None:
   333→        return [], {}
   334→
   335→    graph = lang.dep_graph or lang.build_dep_graph(path)
   336→    extra = find_external_test_files(path, lang)
   337→    entries, potential = detect_test_coverage(
   338→        graph,
   339→        zone_map,
   340→        lang.name,
   341→        extra_test_files=extra or None,
   342→        complexity_map=lang.complexity_map or None,
   343→    )
   344→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "test_coverage")
   345→
   346→    results = _entries_to_findings("test_coverage", entries, default_name="")
   347→    _log_phase_summary("test coverage", results, potential, "production files")
   348→
   349→    return results, {"test_coverage": potential}
   350→
   351→
   352→def phase_private_imports(
   353→    path: Path,
   354→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   355→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   356→    """Shared phase: detect cross-module private imports."""
   357→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   358→    graph = lang.dep_graph or lang.build_dep_graph(path)
   359→
   360→    entries, potential = lang.detect_private_imports(graph, zone_map)
   361→    entries = filter_entries(zone_map, entries, "private_imports")
   362→
   363→    results = _entries_to_findings("private_imports", entries)
   364→    _log_phase_summary("private imports", results, potential, "files scanned")
   365→
   366→    return results, {"private_imports": potential}
   367→
   368→
   369→def phase_subjective_review(
   370→    path: Path,
   371→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   372→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   373→    """Shared phase: detect files missing subjective design review."""
   374→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   375→    max_age = lang.review_max_age_days
   376→    files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
   377→    review_cache = lang.review_cache
   378→    if isinstance(review_cache, dict) and "files" in review_cache:
   379→        per_file_cache = review_cache.get("files", {})
   380→    else:
   381→        # Legacy format: flat dict of file entries with no "files" wrapper.
   382→        # Filter out known top-level structural keys so they aren't treated as
   383→        # file paths, then reconstruct the canonical shape preserving them.
   384→        _TOP_LEVEL_KEYS = frozenset({"holistic"})
   385→        raw = review_cache if isinstance(review_cache, dict) else {}
   386→        per_file_cache = {k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k not in _TOP_LEVEL_KEYS}
   387→        review_cache = {"files": per_file_cache}
   388→        if "holistic" in raw:
   389→            review_cache["holistic"] = raw["holistic"]
   390→
   391→    entries, potential = detect_review_coverage(
   392→        files,
   393→        zone_map,
   394→        per_file_cache,
   395→        lang.name,
   396→        low_value_pattern=lang.review_low_value_pattern,
   397→        max_age_days=max_age,
   398→        holistic_cache=review_cache.get("holistic")
   399→        if isinstance(review_cache, dict)
   400→        else None,
   401→        holistic_total_files=len(files),
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    # Also check holistic review staleness.
   405→    holistic_entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(
   406→        review_cache,
   407→        total_files=len(files),
   408→        max_age_days=max_age,
   409→    )
   410→    entries.extend(holistic_entries)
   411→
   412→    results = _entries_to_findings("subjective_review", entries)
   413→    _log_phase_summary("subjective review", results, potential, "reviewable files")
   414→
   415→    return results, {"subjective_review": potential}
   416→
   417→
   418→def phase_signature(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   419→    """Shared phase runner: detect signature variance via lang.extract_functions.
   420→
   421→    Backend-agnostic — works with any extractor that returns FunctionInfo objects
   422→    (tree-sitter, regex, AST, etc.).  Returns empty results when the lang has no
   423→    function extractor.
   424→    """
   425→    from desloppify.engine.detectors.signature import detect_signature_variance
   426→
   427→    functions = lang.extract_functions(path)
   428→
   429→    findings: list[Finding] = []
   430→    potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
   431→
   432→    if not functions:
   433→        return findings, potentials
   434→
   435→    entries, _total = detect_signature_variance(functions, min_occurrences=3)
   436→    for e in entries:
   437→        findings.append(make_finding(
   438→            "signature", e["files"][0],
   439→            f"signature_variance::{e['name']}",
   440→            tier=3, confidence="medium",
   441→            summary=(
   442→                f"'{e['name']}' has {e['signature_count']} different signatures "
   443→                f"across {e['file_count']} files"
   444→            ),
   445→        ))
   446→    if entries:
   447→        potentials["signature"] = len(entries)
   448→        log(f"         signature variance: {len(entries)}")
   449→
   450→    return findings, potentials
   451→
   452→
   453→def run_structural_phase(
   454→    path: Path,
   455→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   456→    *,
   457→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
   458→    log_fn,
   459→    min_loc: int = 40,
   460→    god_rules=None,
   461→    god_extractor_fn=None,
   462→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   463→    """Run large/complexity/flat directory detectors for a language.
   464→
   465→    Optional ``god_rules`` + ``god_extractor_fn`` enable god-class detection:
   466→    when both are provided, ``god_extractor_fn(path)`` is called to extract
   467→    class info, then ``detect_gods()`` finds classes matching multiple rules.
   468→    """
   469→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
   470→
   471→    large_entries, file_count = detect_large_files(
   472→        path,
   473→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   474→        threshold=lang.large_threshold,
   475→    )
   476→    for entry in large_entries:
   477→        add_structural_signal(
   478→            structural,
   479→            entry["file"],
   480→            f"large ({entry['loc']} LOC)",
   481→            {"loc": entry["loc"]},
   482→        )
   483→
   484→    complexity_entries, _ = detect_complexity(
   485→        path,
   486→        signals=complexity_signals,
   487→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   488→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
   489→        min_loc=min_loc,
   490→    )
   491→    for entry in complexity_entries:
   492→        add_structural_signal(
   493→            structural,
   494→            entry["file"],
   495→            f"complexity score {entry['score']}",
   496→            {"complexity_score": entry["score"], "complexity_signals": entry["signals"]},
   497→        )
   498→        lang.complexity_map[entry["file"]] = entry["score"]
   499→
   500→    if god_rules and god_extractor_fn:
   501→        from desloppify.engine.detectors.gods import detect_gods
   502→
   503→        god_entries, _ = detect_gods(god_extractor_fn(path), god_rules, min_reasons=2)
   504→        for entry in god_entries:
   505→            add_structural_signal(
   506→                structural, entry["file"], entry["signal_text"], entry["detail"],
   507→            )
   508→        if god_entries:
   509→            log_fn(f"         god classes: {len(god_entries)}")
   510→
   511→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log_fn)
   512→    flat_entries, analyzed_dir_count = detect_flat_dirs(path, file_finder=lang.file_finder)
   513→    for entry in flat_entries:
   514→        child_dir_count = int(entry.get("child_dir_count", 0))
   515→        combined_score = int(entry.get("combined_score", entry.get("file_count", 0)))
   516→        results.append(
   517→            make_finding(
   518→                "flat_dirs",
   519→                entry["directory"],
   520→                "",
   521→                tier=3,
   522→                confidence="medium",
   523→                summary=format_flat_dir_summary(entry),
   524→                detail={
   525→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
   526→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   527→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   528→                    "kind": entry.get("kind", "overload"),
   529→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(entry.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   530→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(entry.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   531→                    "sparse_child_count": int(entry.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   532→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(entry.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   533→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   534→                        entry.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   535→                    ),
   536→                },
   537→            )
   538→        )
   539→    if flat_entries:
   540→        log_fn(
   541→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   542→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   543→        )
   544→
   545→    potentials = {
   546→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   547→        "flat_dirs": analyzed_dir_count,
   548→    }
   549→    return results, potentials
   550→
   551→
   552→def run_coupling_phase(
   553→    path: Path,
   554→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   555→    *,
   556→    build_dep_graph_fn,
   557→    log_fn,
   558→    post_process_fn=None,
   559→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   560→    """Run single-use/cycles/orphaned detectors against a language dep graph.
   561→
   562→    Optional ``post_process_fn(findings, entries, lang)`` is called after
   563→    creating single-use and orphaned findings to allow per-language
   564→    adjustments (e.g. confidence gating based on corroboration signals).
   565→    """
   566→    graph = build_dep_graph_fn(path)
   567→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   568→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   569→    results: list[dict] = []
   570→
   571→    single_entries, single_candidates = detect_single_use_abstractions(
   572→        path,
   573→        graph,
   574→        barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   575→    )
   576→    single_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, single_entries, "single_use")
   577→    single_findings = make_single_use_findings(
   578→        single_entries, lang.get_area, stderr_fn=log_fn,
   579→    )
   580→    if post_process_fn:
   581→        post_process_fn(single_findings, single_entries, lang)
   582→    results.extend(single_findings)
   583→
   584→    cycle_entries, _ = detect_cycles(graph)
   585→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   586→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log_fn))
   587→
   588→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = detect_orphaned_files(
   589→        path,
   590→        graph,
   591→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   592→        options=OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   593→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   594→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   595→        ),
   596→    )
   597→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   598→    orphan_findings = make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log_fn)
   599→    if post_process_fn:
   600→        post_process_fn(orphan_findings, orphan_entries, lang)
   601→    results.extend(orphan_findings)
   602→
   603→    log_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   604→    potentials = {
   605→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zone_map, single_candidates),
   606→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   607→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   608→    }
   609→    return results, potentials
   610→
   611→
   612→def make_structural_coupling_phase_pair(
   613→    *,
   614→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
   615→    build_dep_graph_fn,
   616→    log_fn,
   617→) -> tuple:
   618→    """Create default structural/coupling phase callables for a language.
   619→
   620→    This keeps language phase modules declarative by letting them provide only
   621→    their complexity signals and dependency-graph builder.
   622→    """
   623→
   624→    def phase_structural(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   625→        return run_structural_phase(
   626→            path,
   627→            lang,
   628→            complexity_signals=complexity_signals,
   629→            log_fn=log_fn,
   630→        )
   631→
   632→    def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[dict], dict[str, int]]:
   633→        return run_coupling_phase(
   634→            path,
   635→            lang,
   636→            build_dep_graph_fn=build_dep_graph_fn,
   637→            log_fn=log_fn,
   638→        )
   639→
   640→    return phase_structural, phase_coupling
   641→
   642→
   643→__all__ = [
   644→    "find_external_test_files",
   645→    "make_structural_coupling_phase_pair",
   646→    "phase_boilerplate_duplication",
   647→    "phase_dupes",
   648→    "phase_private_imports",
   649→    "phase_security",
   650→    "phase_signature",
   651→    "phase_subjective_review",
   652→    "phase_test_coverage",
   653→    "run_coupling_phase",
   654→    "run_structural_phase",
   655→]
   656→

    40→detector_phase_subjective_review = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["subjective_review"]
    41→detector_phase_duplicates = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["duplicates"]
    42→detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication = SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES["boilerplate_duplication"]
    43→
    44→
    45→def shared_subjective_duplicates_tail(
    46→    *,
    47→    pre_duplicates: list[DetectorPhase] | None = None,
    48→) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    49→    """Shared review tail: subjective review, optional custom phases, then duplicates."""
    50→    phases = [detector_phase_subjective_review()]
    51→    if pre_duplicates:
    52→        phases.extend(pre_duplicates)
    53→    phases.append(detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication())
    54→    phases.append(detector_phase_duplicates())
    55→    return phases
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = [
    59→    "detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication",
    60→    "detector_phase_duplicates",
    61→    "detector_phase_security",
    62→    "detector_phase_signature",
    63→    "detector_phase_subjective_review",
    64→    "detector_phase_test_coverage",
    65→    "SHARED_PHASE_FACTORIES",
    66→    "shared_subjective_duplicates_tail",
    67→]
    68→

   332→
   333→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   334→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   335→)
   336→_BASE_DETECTORS: dict[str, DetectorMeta] = dict(DETECTORS)
   337→_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER: list[str] = list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   338→_BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(JUDGMENT_DETECTORS)
   339→
   340→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   341→
   342→
   343→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   344→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   345→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   346→
   347→
   348→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   349→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   350→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   351→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   352→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   353→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   354→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   355→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   356→    )
   357→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   358→        cb()
   359→
   360→
   361→def reset_registered_detectors() -> None:
   362→    """Reset runtime-added detector registrations to built-in defaults."""
   363→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   364→    DETECTORS.clear()
   365→    DETECTORS.update(_BASE_DETECTORS)
   366→    _DISPLAY_ORDER.clear()
   367→    _DISPLAY_ORDER.extend(_BASE_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   368→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = _BASE_JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   369→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   370→        cb()
   371→
   372→
   373→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   374→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   375→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   376→
   377→
   378→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   379→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   380→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   381→
   382→
   383→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   384→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   385→    "auto_fix": "autofix",
   386→    "reorganize": "move",
   387→    "refactor": "refactor",
   388→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   389→}
   390→
   391→
   392→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   393→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   394→    best = "manual"
   395→    best_pri = 99
   396→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   397→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   398→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   399→            if pri < best_pri:
   400→                best_pri = pri
   401→                best = d.action_type
   402→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   403→
   404→
   405→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   406→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   407→    result = {}
   408→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   409→        entry: dict = {
   410→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   411→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   412→        }
   413→        if d.tool:
   414→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   415→        if d.guidance:
   416→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   417→        result[name] = entry
   418→    return result
   419→

    57→# ── Helper computations for complexity signals ─────────────
    58→
    59→
    60→def _compute_ts_destructure_props(content, lines):
    61→    long_destructures = re.findall(r"\{\s*(\w+(?:\s*,\s*\w+){8,})\s*\}", content)
    62→    if not long_destructures:
    63→        return None
    64→    max_props = max(len(d.split(",")) for d in long_destructures)
    65→    return max_props, f"destructure w/{max_props} props"
    66→
    67→
    68→def _compute_ts_inline_types(content, lines):
    69→    inline_types = len(
    70→        re.findall(r"^(?:export\s+)?(?:type|interface)\s+\w+", content, re.MULTILINE)
    71→    )
    72→    if inline_types > 3:
    73→        return inline_types, f"{inline_types} inline types"
    74→    return None
    75→

   137→def phase_logs(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   138→    log_result = logs_detector_mod.detect_logs_result(path)
   139→    log_entries = log_result.entries

   141→    log_groups: dict[tuple, list] = defaultdict(list)
