```
   143→        log_groups[(e["file"], e["tag"])].append(e)
   144→    results = []
   145→    for (file, tag), entries in log_groups.items():
   146→        results.append(
   147→            make_finding(
   148→                "logs",
   149→                file,
   150→                tag,
   151→                tier=1,
   152→                confidence="high",
   153→                summary=f"{len(entries)} tagged logs [{tag}]",
   154→                detail={
   155→                    "count": len(entries),
   156→                    "lines": [e["line"] for e in entries[:20]],
   157→                },
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→    log(f"         {len(log_entries)} instances → {len(results)} findings")
   161→    return results, {"logs": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def phase_unused(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   165→    entries, total_files = unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path)
   166→    return make_unused_findings(entries, log), {
   167→        "unused": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
   168→    }
   169→
   170→
   171→def phase_exports(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   172→    export_entries, total_exports = exports_detector_mod.detect_dead_exports(path)
   173→    results = []
   174→    for e in export_entries:
   175→        results.append(
   176→            make_finding(
   177→                "exports",
   178→                e["file"],
   179→                e["name"],
   180→                tier=2,
   181→                confidence="high",
   182→                summary=f"Dead export: {e['name']}",
   183→                detail={"line": e.get("line"), "kind": e.get("kind")},
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→    log(f"         {len(export_entries)} instances → {len(results)} findings")
   187→    return results, {"exports": total_exports}
   188→
   189→
   190→def phase_deprecated(
   191→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   192→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   193→    dep_result = deprecated_detector_mod.detect_deprecated_result(path)
   194→    dep_entries = dep_result.entries
   195→    total_deprecated = dep_result.population_size
   196→    results = []
   197→    for e in dep_entries:
   198→        if e["kind"] == "property":
   199→            continue
   200→        tier = 1 if e["importers"] == 0 else 3
   201→        results.append(
   202→            make_finding(
   203→                "deprecated",
   204→                e["file"],
   205→                e["symbol"],
   206→                tier=tier,
   207→                confidence="high",
   208→                summary=f"Deprecated: {e['symbol']} ({e['importers']} importers)"
   209→                + (" → safe to delete" if e["importers"] == 0 else ""),
   210→                detail={"importers": e["importers"], "line": e["line"]},
   211→            )
   212→        )
   213→    log(
   214→        f"         {len(dep_entries)} instances → {len(results)} findings (properties suppressed)"
   215→    )
   216→    return results, {"deprecated": total_deprecated}
   217→
   218→
   219→def phase_structural(
   220→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   221→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   222→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
   223→
   224→    large_entries, file_count = large_detector_mod.detect_large_files(
   225→        path,
   226→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   227→        threshold=lang.large_threshold,
   228→    )
   229→    for e in large_entries:
   230→        add_structural_signal(
   231→            structural, e["file"], f"large ({e['loc']} LOC)", {"loc": e["loc"]}
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    complexity_entries, _ = complexity_detector_mod.detect_complexity(
   235→        path,
   236→        signals=TS_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS,
   237→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   238→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
   239→    )
   240→    for e in complexity_entries:
   241→        add_structural_signal(
   242→            structural,
   243→            e["file"],
   244→            f"complexity score {e['score']}",
   245→            {"complexity_score": e["score"], "complexity_signals": e["signals"]},
   246→        )
   247→        lang.complexity_map[e["file"]] = e["score"]
   248→
   249→    god_entries, _ = gods_detector_mod.detect_gods(
   250→        extract_ts_components(path), TS_GOD_RULES, min_reasons=2
   251→    )
   252→    for e in god_entries:
   253→        add_structural_signal(
   254→            structural,
   255→            e["file"],
   256→            f"{e['detail'].get('hook_total', 0)} hooks ({', '.join(e['reasons'][:2])})",
   257→            {
   258→                "hook_total": e["detail"].get("hook_total", 0),
   259→                "hook_reasons": e["reasons"],
   260→            },
   261→        )
   262→
   263→    concern_entries, _ = concerns_detector_mod.detect_mixed_concerns(path)
   264→    for e in concern_entries:
   265→        add_structural_signal(
   266→            structural,
   267→            e["file"],
   268→            f"mixed: {', '.join(e['concerns'][:3])}",
   269→            {"concerns": e["concerns"]},
   270→        )
   271→
   272→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log)
   273→
   274→    # Flat directories (too many files → missing sub-organization)
   275→    flat_entries, dir_count = flat_dirs_detector_mod.detect_flat_dirs(
   276→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder
   277→    )
   278→    for e in flat_entries:
   279→        child_dir_count = int(e.get("child_dir_count", 0))
   280→        combined_score = int(e.get("combined_score", e.get("file_count", 0)))
   281→        results.append(
   282→            make_finding(
   283→                "flat_dirs",
   284→                e["directory"],
   285→                "",
   286→                tier=3,
   287→                confidence="medium",
   288→                summary=flat_dirs_detector_mod.format_flat_dir_summary(e),
   289→                detail={
   290→                    "file_count": e["file_count"],
   291→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   292→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   293→                    "kind": e.get("kind", "overload"),
   294→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(e.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   295→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(e.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   296→                    "sparse_child_count": int(e.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   297→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(e.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   298→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   299→                        e.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   300→                    ),
   301→                },
   302→            )
   303→        )
   304→    if flat_entries:
   305→        log(
   306→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   307→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   308→        )
   309→
   310→    # TS-specific: props bloat
   311→    props_thresh = lang.props_threshold
   312→    prop_entries, prop_count = props_detector_mod.detect_prop_interface_bloat(
   313→        path, threshold=props_thresh
   314→    )
   315→    for e in prop_entries:
   316→        pc = e["prop_count"]
   317→        # Tiered severity: 15-29=low, 30-49=medium, 50+=high
   318→        if pc >= 50:
   319→            conf, tier = "high", 4
   320→        elif pc >= 30:
   321→            conf, tier = "medium", 3
   322→        else:
   323→            conf, tier = "low", 3
   324→        results.append(
   325→            make_finding(
   326→                "props",
   327→                e["file"],
   328→                e["interface"],
   329→                tier=tier,
   330→                confidence=conf,
   331→                summary=f"Bloated {e.get('kind', 'props')}: {e['interface']} ({pc} fields)",
   332→                detail={
   333→                    "prop_count": pc,
   334→                    "line": e["line"],
   335→                    "kind": e.get("kind", "props"),
   336→                },
   337→            )
   338→        )
   339→
   340→    # TS-specific: passthrough components
   341→    pt_entries = detect_passthrough_components(path)
   342→    for e in pt_entries:
   343→        results.append(
   344→            make_finding(
   345→                "props",
   346→                e["file"],
   347→                f"passthrough::{e['component']}",
   348→                tier=e["tier"],
   349→                confidence=e["confidence"],
   350→                summary=f"Passthrough component: {e['component']} "
   351→                f"({e['passthrough']}/{e['total_props']} props forwarded, {e['ratio']:.0%})",
   352→                detail={
   353→                    "passthrough": e["passthrough"],
   354→                    "total_props": e["total_props"],
   355→                    "ratio": e["ratio"],
   356→                    "line": e["line"],
   357→                    "passthrough_props": e["passthrough_props"],
   358→                    "direct_props": e["direct_props"],
   359→                },
   360→            )
   361→        )
   362→    potentials = {
   363→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   364→        "flat_dirs": dir_count,
   365→        "props": max(prop_count, len(pt_entries)) if prop_count else len(pt_entries),
   366→    }
   367→    return results, potentials
   368→
   369→
   370→def _make_boundary_findings(
   371→    single_entries: list[dict],
   372→    path: Path,
   373→    graph: dict,
   374→    lang: LangRun,
   375→    shared_prefix: str,
   376→    tools_prefix: str,
   377→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   378→    """Create boundary-candidate findings, deduplicated against single-use."""
   379→    single_use_emitted = set()
   380→    for e in single_entries:
   381→        is_size_ok = 50 <= e["loc"] <= 200
   382→        is_colocated = lang.get_area and (
   383→            lang.get_area(rel(e["file"])) == lang.get_area(e["sole_importer"])
   384→        )
   385→        if not is_size_ok and not is_colocated:
   386→            single_use_emitted.add(rel(e["file"]))
   387→
   388→    results = []
   389→    deduped = 0
   390→    boundary_entries, total_shared = coupling_detector_mod.detect_boundary_candidates(
   391→        path,
   392→        graph,
   393→        shared_prefix=shared_prefix,
   394→        tools_prefix=tools_prefix,
   395→        skip_basenames={"index.ts", "index.tsx"},
   396→    )
   397→    for e in boundary_entries:
   398→        if rel(e["file"]) in single_use_emitted:
   399→            deduped += 1
   400→            continue
   401→        results.append(
   402→            make_finding(
   403→                "coupling",
   404→                e["file"],
   405→                f"boundary::{e['sole_tool']}",
   406→                tier=3,
   407→                confidence="medium",
   408→                summary=f"Boundary candidate ({e['loc']} LOC): only used by {e['sole_tool']} "
   409→                f"({e['importer_count']} importers)",
   410→                detail={
   411→                    "sole_tool": e["sole_tool"],
   412→                    "importer_count": e["importer_count"],
   413→                    "loc": e["loc"],
   414→                },
   415→            )
   416→        )
   417→    if deduped:
   418→        log(f"         ({deduped} boundary candidates skipped — covered by single_use)")
   419→    return results, total_shared
   420→
   421→
   422→def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   423→    results = []
   424→    graph = deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph(path)
   425→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   426→    zm = lang.zone_map
   427→
   428→    # Single-use (shared helper)
   429→    single_entries, single_candidates = (
   430→        single_use_detector_mod.detect_single_use_abstractions(
   431→            path, graph, barrel_names=lang.barrel_names
   432→        )
   433→    )
   434→    single_entries = filter_entries(zm, single_entries, "single_use")
   435→    results.extend(
   436→        make_single_use_findings(
   437→            single_entries, lang.get_area, skip_dir_names={"commands"}, stderr_fn=log
   438→        )
   439→    )
   440→    src_path = get_src_path()
   441→    shared_prefix = f"{src_path}/shared/"
   442→    tools_prefix = f"{src_path}/tools/"
   443→    coupling_entries, coupling_edge_counts = coupling_detector_mod.detect_coupling_violations(
   444→        path, graph, shared_prefix=shared_prefix, tools_prefix=tools_prefix
   445→    )
   446→    coupling_entries = filter_entries(zm, coupling_entries, "coupling")
   447→    for e in coupling_entries:
   448→        results.append(
   449→            make_finding(
   450→                "coupling",
   451→                e["file"],
   452→                e["target"],
   453→                tier=2,
   454→                confidence="high",
   455→                summary=f"Backwards coupling: shared imports {e['target']} (tool: {e['tool']})",
   456→                detail={
   457→                    "target": e["target"],
   458→                    "tool": e["tool"],
   459→                    "direction": e["direction"],
   460→                },
   461→            )
   462→        )
   463→
   464→    # TS-specific: boundary candidates (deduplicated against single-use)
   465→    boundary_findings, _ = _make_boundary_findings(
   466→        single_entries, path, graph, lang, shared_prefix, tools_prefix
   467→    )
   468→    results.extend(boundary_findings)
   469→
   470→    # TS-specific: cross-tool imports
   471→    cross_tool, cross_edge_counts = coupling_detector_mod.detect_cross_tool_imports(
   472→        path, graph, tools_prefix=tools_prefix
   473→    )
   474→    cross_tool = filter_entries(zm, cross_tool, "coupling")
   475→    for e in cross_tool:
   476→        results.append(
   477→            make_finding(
   478→                "coupling",
   479→                e["file"],
   480→                e["target"],
   481→                tier=2,
   482→                confidence="high",
   483→                summary=f"Cross-tool import: {e['source_tool']}→{e['target_tool']} ({e['target']})",
   484→                detail={
   485→                    "target": e["target"],
   486→                    "source_tool": e["source_tool"],
   487→                    "target_tool": e["target_tool"],
   488→                    "direction": e["direction"],
   489→                },
   490→            )
   491→        )
   492→    if cross_tool:
   493→        log(f"         cross-tool: {len(cross_tool)} imports")
   494→
   495→    # Cycles + orphaned (shared helpers)
   496→    cycle_entries, _ = graph_detector_mod.detect_cycles(graph)
   497→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zm, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   498→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log))
   499→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = orphaned_detector_mod.detect_orphaned_files(
   500→        path,
   501→        graph,
   502→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   503→        options=orphaned_detector_mod.OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   504→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   505→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   506→            dynamic_import_finder=deps_detector_mod.build_dynamic_import_targets,
   507→            alias_resolver=deps_detector_mod.ts_alias_resolver,
   508→        ),
   509→    )
   510→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zm, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   511→    results.extend(make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log))
   512→
   513→    # Re-export facades (shared detector)
   514→    facade_entries, _ = facade_detector_mod.detect_reexport_facades(graph)
   515→    facade_entries = filter_entries(zm, facade_entries, "facade")
   516→    results.extend(make_facade_findings(facade_entries, log))
   517→
   518→    # TS-specific: pattern consistency
   519→    pattern_result = patterns_detector_mod.detect_pattern_anomalies_result(path)
   520→    pattern_entries = pattern_result.entries
   521→    total_areas = pattern_result.population_size
   522→    for e in pattern_entries:
   523→        results.append(
   524→            make_finding(
   525→                "patterns",
   526→                e["area"],
   527→                e["family"],
   528→                tier=3,
   529→                confidence=e.get("confidence", "low"),
   530→                summary=f"Competing patterns ({e['family']}): {e['review'][:120]}",
   531→                detail={
   532→                    "family": e["family"],
   533→                    "patterns_used": e["patterns_used"],
   534→                    "pattern_count": e["pattern_count"],
   535→                    "review": e["review"],
   536→                },
   537→            )
   538→        )
   539→
   540→    # TS-specific: naming consistency
   541→    naming_entries, total_dirs = naming_detector_mod.detect_naming_inconsistencies(
   542→        path,
   543→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   544→        skip_names=TS_SKIP_NAMES,
   545→        skip_dirs=TS_SKIP_DIRS,
   546→    )
   547→    for e in naming_entries:
   548→        results.append(
   549→            make_finding(
   550→                "naming",
   551→                e["directory"],
   552→                e["minority"],
   553→                tier=3,
   554→                confidence="low",
   555→                summary=f"Naming inconsistency: {e['minority_count']} {e['minority']} files "
   556→                f"in {e['majority']}-majority dir ({e['total_files']} total)",
   557→                detail={
   558→                    "majority": e["majority"],
   559→                    "majority_count": e["majority_count"],
   560→                    "minority": e["minority"],
   561→                    "minority_count": e["minority_count"],
   562→                    "outliers": e["outliers"],
   563→                },
   564→            )
   565→        )
   566→    log(f"         → {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   567→    coupling_edges = coupling_edge_counts.eligible_edges
   568→    cross_edges = cross_edge_counts.eligible_edges
   569→    potentials = {
   570→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zm, single_candidates),
   571→        "coupling": coupling_edges + cross_edges,
   572→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   573→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   574→        "patterns": total_areas,
   575→        "naming": total_dirs,
   576→        "facade": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   577→    }
   578→    return results, potentials
   579→
   580→
   581→def phase_smells(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   582→    smell_entries, total_smell_files = smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)
   583→    results = make_smell_findings(smell_entries, log)
   584→
   585→    # TS-specific: React state sync anti-patterns
   586→    react_entries, total_effects = react_detector_mod.detect_state_sync(path)
   587→    for e in react_entries:
   588→        setter_str = ", ".join(e["setters"])
   589→        results.append(
   590→            make_finding(
   591→                "react",
   592→                e["file"],
   593→                setter_str,
   594→                tier=3,
   595→                confidence="medium",
   596→                summary=f"State sync anti-pattern: useEffect only calls {setter_str}",
   597→                detail={"line": e["line"], "setters": e["setters"]},
   598→            )
   599→        )
   600→    if react_entries:
   601→        log(f"         react: {len(react_entries)} state sync anti-patterns")
   602→
   603→    # TS-specific: Context provider nesting depth
   604→    nesting_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_context_nesting(path)
   605→    for e in nesting_entries:
   606→        providers_str = " → ".join(e["providers"][:5])
   607→        results.append(
   608→            make_finding(
   609→                "react",
   610→                e["file"],
   611→                f"nesting::{e['depth']}",
   612→                tier=3,
   613→                confidence="medium",
   614→                summary=f"Deep provider nesting ({e['depth']} levels): {providers_str}",
   615→                detail={"depth": e["depth"], "providers": e["providers"]},
   616→            )
   617→        )
   618→    if nesting_entries:
   619→        log(f"         react: {len(nesting_entries)} deep provider nesting")
   620→
   621→    # TS-specific: Hook return bloat
   622→    hook_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_hook_return_bloat(path)
   623→    for e in hook_entries:
   624→        results.append(
   625→            make_finding(
   626→                "react",
   627→                e["file"],
   628→                f"hook_bloat::{e['hook']}",
   629→                tier=3,
   630→                confidence="medium",
   631→                summary=f"Hook return bloat: {e['hook']} returns {e['field_count']} fields",
   632→                detail={
   633→                    "hook": e["hook"],
   634→                    "field_count": e["field_count"],
   635→                    "line": e["line"],
   636→                },
   637→            )
   638→        )
   639→    if hook_entries:
   640→        log(f"         react: {len(hook_entries)} bloated hook returns")
   641→
   642→    # TS-specific: Boolean state explosion
   643→    bool_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_boolean_state_explosion(path)
   644→    for e in bool_entries:
   645→        states_str = ", ".join(e["states"][:5])
   646→        results.append(
   647→            make_finding(
   648→                "react",
   649→                e["file"],
   650→                f"bool_state::{e['prefix']}",
   651→                tier=3,
   652→                confidence="low",
   653→                summary=f"Boolean state explosion: {e['count']} boolean useState hooks ({states_str})",
   654→                detail={
   655→                    "count": e["count"],
   656→                    "setters": e["setters"],
   657→                    "states": e["states"],
   658→                    "line": e["line"],
   659→                },
   660→            )
   661→        )
   662→    if bool_entries:
   663→        log(f"         react: {len(bool_entries)} boolean state explosions")
   664→
   665→    return results, {
   666→        "smells": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_smell_files),
   667→        "react": total_effects,
   668→    }
   669→
   670→
   671→# Backward-compatible aliases for internal callers/tests.
   672→_phase_logs = phase_logs
   673→_phase_unused = phase_unused
   674→_phase_exports = phase_exports
   675→_phase_deprecated = phase_deprecated
   676→_phase_structural = phase_structural
   677→_phase_coupling = phase_coupling
   678→_phase_smells = phase_smells
   679→
```

```
    25→    run_phase_coupling,
    26→    run_phase_structural,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.languages.python.phases_quality import (
    29→    phase_dict_keys,
    30→    phase_layer_violation,
    31→    phase_mutable_state,
    32→    phase_smells,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.state import Finding
    35→from desloppify.core.output_api import log
    36→
    37→# ── Config data (single source of truth) ──────────────────
    38→
    39→
    40→PY_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS = [
    41→    ComplexitySignal("imports", r"^(?:import |from )", weight=1, threshold=20),
    42→    ComplexitySignal(
    43→        "many_params", None, weight=2, threshold=7, compute=compute_max_params
    44→    ),
    45→    ComplexitySignal(
    46→        "deep_nesting", None, weight=3, threshold=4, compute=compute_nesting_depth
    47→    ),
    48→    ComplexitySignal(
    49→        "long_functions", None, weight=1, threshold=80, compute=compute_long_functions
    50→    ),
    51→    ComplexitySignal("many_classes", r"^class\s+\w+", weight=3, threshold=3),
    52→    ComplexitySignal(
    53→        "nested_comprehensions",
    54→        r"\[[^\]]*\bfor\b[^\]]*\bfor\b[^\]]*\]|\{[^}]*\bfor\b[^}]*\bfor\b[^}]*\}",
    55→        weight=2,
    56→        threshold=2,
    57→    ),
    58→    ComplexitySignal("TODOs", r"#\s*(?:TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX)", weight=2, threshold=0),
    59→]
    60→
    61→PY_GOD_RULES = [
    62→    GodRule("methods", "methods", lambda c: len(c.methods), 15),
    63→    GodRule("attributes", "attributes", lambda c: len(c.attributes), 10),
    64→    GodRule("base_classes", "base classes", lambda c: len(c.base_classes), 3),
    65→    GodRule(
    66→        "long_methods",
    67→        "long methods (>50 LOC)",
    68→        lambda c: sum(1 for m in c.methods if m.loc > 50),
    69→        1,
    70→    ),
    71→]
    72→
    73→PY_SKIP_NAMES = {
    74→    "__init__.py",
    75→    "conftest.py",
    76→    "setup.py",
    77→    "manage.py",
    78→    "__main__.py",
    79→    "wsgi.py",
    80→    "asgi.py",
    81→}
    82→
    83→PY_ENTRY_PATTERNS = [
    84→    "__main__.py",
    85→    "conftest.py",
    86→    "manage.py",
    87→    "setup.py",
    88→    "setup.cfg",
    89→    "test_",
    90→    "_test.py",
    91→    ".test.",
    92→    "/tests/",
    93→    "/test/",
    94→    "/migrations/",
    95→    "settings.py",
    96→    "config.py",
    97→    "wsgi.py",
    98→    "asgi.py",
    99→    "cli.py",  # CLI entry points (loaded via framework/importlib)
   100→    "/commands/",  # CLI subcommands (loaded dynamically)
   101→    "/fixers/",  # Fixer modules (loaded dynamically)
   102→    "/lang/",  # Language modules (loaded dynamically)
   103→    "/extractors/",  # Extractor modules (loaded dynamically)
   104→    "__init__.py",  # Package init files (barrels, not orphans)
   105→]
   106→
   107→
   108→def phase_unused(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   109→    entries, total_files = unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path)
   110→    return make_unused_findings(entries, log), {
   111→        "unused": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
   112→    }
   113→
   114→
   115→def phase_structural(
   116→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   117→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   118→    return run_phase_structural(
   119→        path,
   120→        lang,
   121→        complexity_signals=PY_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS,
   122→        god_rules=PY_GOD_RULES,
   123→        log_fn=log,
   124→    )
   125→
   126→
   127→def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   128→    return run_phase_coupling(path, lang, log_fn=log)
   129→
   130→
   131→def phase_responsibility_cohesion(
   132→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   133→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   134→    entries, candidates = cohesion_detector_mod.detect_responsibility_cohesion(path)
   135→    entries = filter_entries(lang.zone_map, entries, "responsibility_cohesion")
   136→
   137→    results: list[dict] = []
   138→    for entry in entries:
   139→        comp_sizes = ", ".join(str(size) for size in entry["component_sizes"][:5])
   140→        if len(entry["component_sizes"]) > 5:
   141→            comp_sizes += f", +{len(entry['component_sizes']) - 5} more"
   142→        results.append(
   143→            state_mod.make_finding(
   144→                "responsibility_cohesion",
   145→                entry["file"],
   146→                "",
   147→                tier=3,
   148→                confidence="medium",
   149→                summary=(
   150→                    f"Mixed responsibilities: {entry['function_count']} top-level funcs "
   151→                    f"across {entry['component_count']} disconnected clusters "
   152→                    f"({comp_sizes})"
   153→                ),
   154→                detail={
   155→                    "loc": entry["loc"],
   156→                    "function_count": entry["function_count"],
   157→                    "component_count": entry["component_count"],
   158→                    "component_sizes": entry["component_sizes"],
   159→                    "family_count": entry["family_count"],
   160→                    "import_cluster_count": entry["import_cluster_count"],
   161→                    "families": entry["families"],
   162→                },
   163→            )
   164→        )
   165→
   166→    if results:
   167→        log(f"         responsibility cohesion: {len(results)} modules")
   168→    return results, {
   169→        "responsibility_cohesion": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, candidates)
   170→    }
   171→
   172→def phase_uncalled_functions(
   173→    path: Path, lang: LangRun
   174→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   175→    """Detect underscore-prefixed top-level functions with zero references."""
   176→    entries, total = uncalled_detector_mod.detect_uncalled_functions(
   177→        path, lang.dep_graph
   178→    )
   179→    zm = lang.zone_map
   180→    entries = filter_entries(zm, entries, "uncalled_functions")
   181→
   182→    results: list[Finding] = []
   183→    for entry in entries:
   184→        results.append(
   185→            state_mod.make_finding(
   186→                "uncalled_functions",
   187→                entry["file"],
   188→                entry["name"],
   189→                tier=3,
   190→                confidence="high",
   191→                summary=f"Uncalled private function: {entry['name']}() — {entry['loc']} LOC, zero references",
   192→                detail={"line": entry["line"], "loc": entry["loc"]},
   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→
   196→    if results:
   197→        log(f"         uncalled functions: {len(results)} dead private functions")
   198→    return results, {"uncalled_functions": adjust_potential(zm, total)}
   199→
   200→
   201→# Backward-compatible aliases for internal callers/tests.
   202→_phase_unused = phase_unused
   203→_phase_structural = phase_structural
   204→_phase_coupling = phase_coupling
   205→_phase_responsibility_cohesion = phase_responsibility_cohesion
   206→_phase_uncalled_functions = phase_uncalled_functions
   207→_phase_smells = phase_smells
   208→_phase_mutable_state = phase_mutable_state
   209→_phase_layer_violation = phase_layer_violation
   210→_phase_dict_keys = phase_dict_keys
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "PY_COMPLEXITY_SIGNALS",
   215→    "PY_ENTRY_PATTERNS",
   216→    "PY_GOD_RULES",
   217→    "PY_SKIP_NAMES",
   218→    "phase_coupling",
   219→    "phase_dict_keys",
   220→    "phase_layer_violation",
   221→    "phase_mutable_state",
   222→    "phase_responsibility_cohesion",
   223→    "phase_smells",
   224→    "phase_structural",
   225→    "phase_uncalled_functions",
   226→    "phase_unused",
   227→]
   228→
```

```
     2→

    23→def phase_smells(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    24→    """Run file/code smell detectors plus cross-file signature variance."""
    25→    entries, total_files = smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)
    26→    # Supplement with ruff B/E/W rules not covered by the regex smells above.
    27→    ruff_entries = detect_with_ruff_smells(path)
    28→    if ruff_entries:
    29→        entries = entries + ruff_entries
    30→    results = make_smell_findings(entries, log)
    31→
    32→    return results, {
    33→        "smells": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
    34→    }
    35→
    36→
    37→def phase_mutable_state(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Find global mutable config patterns."""
    39→    entries, total_files = mutable_state_detector_mod.detect_global_mutable_config(path)
    40→    results = []
    41→    for entry in entries:
    42→        results.append(
    43→            state_mod.make_finding(
    44→                "global_mutable_config",
    45→                entry["file"],
    46→                entry["name"],
    47→                tier=3,
    48→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
    49→                summary=entry["summary"],
    50→                detail={
    51→                    "mutation_count": entry["mutation_count"],
    52→                    "mutation_lines": entry["mutation_lines"],
    53→                },
    54→            )
    55→        )
    56→    if results:
    57→        log(f"         global mutable config: {len(results)} findings")
    58→    return results, {
    59→        "global_mutable_config": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
    60→    }
    61→
    62→
    63→def phase_layer_violation(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    64→    """Find package/layer boundary violations via import-linter."""
    65→    entries = import_linter_adapter_mod.detect_with_import_linter(path)
    66→    if entries is None:
    67→        return [], {}
    68→
    69→    total_files = len(lang.file_finder(path)) if lang.file_finder else 0
    70→    results = []
    71→    for entry in entries:
    72→        results.append(
    73→            state_mod.make_finding(
    74→                "layer_violation",
    75→                entry["file"],
    76→                f"{entry['source_pkg']}::{entry['target_pkg']}",
    77→                tier=2,
    78→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
    79→                summary=entry["summary"],
    80→                detail={
    81→                    "source_pkg": entry["source_pkg"],
    82→                    "target_pkg": entry["target_pkg"],
    83→                    "line": entry.get("line", 0),
    84→                    "description": entry.get("summary", ""),
    85→                },
    86→            )
    87→        )
    88→    if results:
    89→        log(f"         layer violations: {len(results)} findings")
    90→    return results, {"layer_violation": total_files}
    91→
    92→
    93→def phase_dict_keys(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    94→    """Run dict-key flow and schema-drift analysis."""
    95→    flow_entries, files_checked = dict_keys_detector_mod.detect_dict_key_flow(path)
    96→    flow_entries = filter_entries(lang.zone_map, flow_entries, "dict_keys")
    97→
    98→    results = []
    99→    for entry in flow_entries:
   100→        results.append(
   101→            state_mod.make_finding(
   102→                "dict_keys",
   103→                entry["file"],
   104→                f"{entry['kind']}::{entry['variable']}::{entry['key']}"
   105→                if "variable" in entry
   106→                else f"{entry['kind']}::{entry['key']}::{entry['line']}",
   107→                tier=entry["tier"],
   108→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
   109→                summary=entry["summary"],
   110→                detail={
   111→                    "kind": entry["kind"],
   112→                    "key": entry.get("key", ""),
   113→                    "line": entry.get("line"),
   114→                    "info": entry.get("detail", ""),
   115→                },
   116→            )
   117→        )
   118→
   119→    drift_entries, _ = dict_keys_detector_mod.detect_schema_drift(path)
   120→    drift_entries = filter_entries(lang.zone_map, drift_entries, "dict_keys")
   121→    for entry in drift_entries:
   122→        results.append(
   123→            state_mod.make_finding(
   124→                "dict_keys",
   125→                entry["file"],
   126→                f"schema_drift::{entry['key']}::{entry['line']}",
   127→                tier=entry["tier"],
   128→                confidence=entry["confidence"],
   129→                summary=entry["summary"],
   130→                detail={
   131→                    "kind": "schema_drift",
   132→                    "key": entry["key"],
   133→                    "line": entry["line"],
   134→                    "info": entry.get("detail", ""),
   135→                },
   136→            )
   137→        )
   138→
   139→    log(f"         -> {len(results)} dict key findings")
   140→    return results, {
   141→        "dict_keys": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, files_checked),
   142→    }
   143→
```

```
1→"""Extracted heavy phase implementations for Python language pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.engine.detectors import complexity as complexity_detector_mod
     9→from desloppify.engine.detectors import flat_dirs as flat_dirs_detector_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.detectors import gods as gods_detector_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.detectors import graph as graph_detector_mod
    12→from desloppify.engine.detectors import large as large_detector_mod
    13→from desloppify.engine.detectors import orphaned as orphaned_detector_mod
    14→from desloppify.engine.detectors import single_use as single_use_detector_mod
    15→from desloppify.engine.detectors.base import ComplexitySignal, GodRule
    16→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import adjust_potential, filter_entries
    17→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.structural import (
    18→    add_structural_signal,
    19→    merge_structural_signals,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.languages._framework.finding_factories import (
    22→    make_cycle_findings,
    23→    make_facade_findings,
    24→    make_orphaned_findings,
    25→    make_passthrough_findings,
    26→    make_single_use_findings,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun
    29→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors import (
    30→    coupling_contracts as coupling_contracts_detector_mod,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors import deps as deps_detector_mod
    33→from desloppify.languages.python.detectors import facade as facade_detector_mod
    34→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors import detect_passthrough_functions
    35→from desloppify.languages.python.extractors_classes import extract_py_classes
    36→from desloppify.state import Finding
    37→
    38→
    39→def run_phase_structural(
    40→    path: Path,
    41→    lang: LangRun,
    42→    *,
    43→    complexity_signals: list[ComplexitySignal],
    44→    god_rules: list[GodRule],
    45→    log_fn,
    46→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    47→    """Merge large + complexity + god classes into structural findings."""
    48→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
    49→
    50→    large_entries, file_count = large_detector_mod.detect_large_files(
    51→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder, threshold=lang.large_threshold
    52→    )
    53→    for entry in large_entries:
    54→        add_structural_signal(
    55→            structural,
    56→            entry["file"],
    57→            f"large ({entry['loc']} LOC)",
    58→            {"loc": entry["loc"]},
    59→        )
    60→
    61→    complexity_entries, _ = complexity_detector_mod.detect_complexity(
    62→        path,
    63→        signals=complexity_signals,
    64→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
    65→        threshold=lang.complexity_threshold,
    66→    )
    67→    for entry in complexity_entries:
    68→        add_structural_signal(
    69→            structural,
    70→            entry["file"],
    71→            f"complexity score {entry['score']}",
    72→            {"complexity_score": entry["score"], "complexity_signals": entry["signals"]},
    73→        )
    74→        lang.complexity_map[entry["file"]] = entry["score"]
    75→
    76→    god_entries, _ = gods_detector_mod.detect_gods(extract_py_classes(path), god_rules)
    77→    for entry in god_entries:
    78→        add_structural_signal(
    79→            structural,
    80→            entry["file"],
    81→            entry["signal_text"],
    82→            entry["detail"],
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log_fn)
    86→
    87→    flat_entries, dir_count = flat_dirs_detector_mod.detect_flat_dirs(
    88→        path, file_finder=lang.file_finder
    89→    )
    90→    for entry in flat_entries:
    91→        child_dir_count = int(entry.get("child_dir_count", 0))
    92→        combined_score = int(entry.get("combined_score", entry.get("file_count", 0)))
    93→        results.append(
    94→            state_mod.make_finding(
    95→                "flat_dirs",
    96→                entry["directory"],
    97→                "",
    98→                tier=3,
    99→                confidence="medium",
   100→                summary=flat_dirs_detector_mod.format_flat_dir_summary(entry),
   101→                detail={
   102→                    "file_count": entry["file_count"],
   103→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   104→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   105→                    "kind": entry.get("kind", "overload"),
   106→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(entry.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   107→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(entry.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   108→                    "sparse_child_count": int(entry.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   109→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(entry.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   110→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   111→                        entry.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   112→                    ),
   113→                },
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    if flat_entries:
   117→        log_fn(
   118→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   119→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   120→        )
   121→
   122→    passthrough_entries = detect_passthrough_functions(path)
   123→    results.extend(
   124→        make_passthrough_findings(
   125→            passthrough_entries,
   126→            "function",
   127→            "total_params",
   128→            log_fn,
   129→        )
   130→    )
   131→
   132→    potentials = {
   133→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   134→        "flat_dirs": dir_count,
   135→        "props": len(passthrough_entries) if passthrough_entries else 0,
   136→    }
   137→    return results, potentials
   138→
   139→
   140→def run_phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRun, *, log_fn) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   141→    """Run coupling-related detectors and return findings/potentials."""
   142→    graph = deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph(path)
   143→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   144→    zone_map = lang.zone_map
   145→
   146→    single_entries, single_candidates = (
   147→        single_use_detector_mod.detect_single_use_abstractions(
   148→            path, graph, barrel_names=lang.barrel_names
   149→        )
   150→    )
   151→    single_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, single_entries, "single_use")
   152→    results = make_single_use_findings(
   153→        single_entries, lang.get_area, skip_dir_names={"commands"}, stderr_fn=log_fn
   154→    )
   155→
   156→    cycle_entries, _ = graph_detector_mod.detect_cycles(graph)
   157→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   158→    results.extend(make_cycle_findings(cycle_entries, log_fn))
   159→
   160→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = orphaned_detector_mod.detect_orphaned_files(
   161→        path,
   162→        graph,
   163→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   164→        options=orphaned_detector_mod.OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   165→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   166→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   167→            dynamic_import_finder=deps_detector_mod.find_python_dynamic_imports,
   168→        ),
   169→    )
   170→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   171→    results.extend(make_orphaned_findings(orphan_entries, log_fn))
   172→
   173→    facade_entries, _ = facade_detector_mod.detect_reexport_facades(graph)
   174→    facade_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, facade_entries, "facade")
   175→    results.extend(make_facade_findings(facade_entries, log_fn))
   176→
   177→    mixin_entries, coupling_candidates = (
   178→        coupling_contracts_detector_mod.detect_implicit_mixin_contracts(path)
   179→    )
   180→    mixin_entries = filter_entries(zone_map, mixin_entries, "coupling")
   181→    for entry in mixin_entries:
   182→        attr_preview = ", ".join(entry["required_attrs"][:4])
   183→        if len(entry["required_attrs"]) > 4:
   184→            attr_preview += f", +{len(entry['required_attrs']) - 4} more"
   185→        required_count = entry["required_count"]
   186→        tier = 4 if required_count >= 6 else 3
   187→        confidence = "high" if required_count >= 6 else "medium"
   188→        results.append(
   189→            state_mod.make_finding(
   190→                "coupling",
   191→                entry["file"],
   192→                entry["class"],
   193→                tier=tier,
   194→                confidence=confidence,
   195→                summary=(
   196→                    f"Implicit host contract: {entry['class']} depends on {required_count} "
   197→                    f"undeclared self attrs ({attr_preview})"
   198→                ),
   199→                detail={
   200→                    "subtype": "implicit_mixin_contract",
   201→                    "required_attrs": entry["required_attrs"],
   202→                    "required_count": required_count,
   203→                    "line": entry.get("line"),
   204→                },
   205→            )
   206→        )
   207→
   208→    log_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} coupling/structural findings total")
   209→    potentials = {
   210→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zone_map, single_candidates),
   211→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   212→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   213→        "facade": adjust_potential(zone_map, total_graph_files),
   214→        "coupling": adjust_potential(zone_map, coupling_candidates),
   215→    }
   216→    return results, potentials
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = ["run_phase_coupling", "run_phase_structural"]
   220→
```

```
    20→def _compute_max_nesting(content: str, _lines: list[str]):
    21→    depth = 0
    22→    max_depth = 0
    23→    for ch in content:
    24→        if ch == "{":
    25→            depth += 1
    26→            max_depth = max(max_depth, depth)
    27→        elif ch == "}":
    28→            depth = max(0, depth - 1)
    29→    if max_depth > 8:
    30→        return max_depth, f"nesting depth {max_depth}"
    31→    return None
    32→
    33→
    34→def _compute_method_count(content: str, _lines: list[str]):
    35→    count = len(
    36→        re.findall(
    37→            r"(?m)^[ \t]*(?:(?:public|private|protected|internal|static|virtual|override|abstract|"
    38→            r"sealed|partial|async|extern|unsafe|new|required)\s+)+"
    39→            r"(?:[\w<>\[\],\.\?]+\s+)+[A-Za-z_]\w*\s*\(",
    40→            content,
    41→        )
    42→    )
    43→    if count > 20:
    44→        return count, f"{count} methods"
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def _compute_long_methods(content: str, _lines: list[str]):
```

```
1→"""Large file detection (LOC threshold)."""
     2→
     3→import logging
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.file_paths import resolve_scan_file
     8→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
     9→
    10→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    11→
    12→
    13→def detect_large_files(
    14→    path: Path, file_finder, threshold: int = 500
    15→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    16→    """Find files exceeding a line count threshold."""
    17→    files = file_finder(path)
    18→    entries = []
    19→    for filepath in files:
    20→        try:
    21→            p = resolve_scan_file(filepath, scan_root=path)
    22→            loc = len(p.read_text().splitlines())
    23→            if loc > threshold:
    24→                entries.append({"file": filepath, "loc": loc})
    25→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    26→            log_best_effort_failure(
    27→                logger,
    28→                f"read large-file detector candidate {filepath}",
    29→                exc,
    30→            )
    31→            continue
    32→    return sorted(entries, key=lambda e: -e["loc"]), len(files)
    33→
```

```
1→"""Complexity signal detection: configurable per-language complexity signals."""
     2→
     3→import inspect
     4→import logging
     5→import re
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
     9→from desloppify.core.file_paths import resolve_scan_file
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def detect_complexity(
    15→    path: Path, signals, file_finder, threshold: int = 15, min_loc: int = 50
    16→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    17→    """Detect files with complexity signals.
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        path: Directory to scan.
    21→        signals: list of ComplexitySignal objects. Required.
    22→        file_finder: callable(path) -> list[str]. Required.
    23→        threshold: minimum score to flag a file.
    24→        min_loc: minimum LOC to consider.
    25→
    26→    Returns:
    27→        (entries, total_files_checked)
    28→    """
    29→    files = file_finder(path)
    30→    entries = []
    31→    for filepath in files:
    32→        try:
    33→            p = resolve_scan_file(filepath, scan_root=path)
    34→            content = p.read_text()
    35→            lines = content.splitlines()
    36→            loc = len(lines)
    37→            if loc < min_loc:
    38→                continue
    39→
    40→            file_signals = []
    41→            score = 0
    42→
    43→            for sig in signals:
    44→                try:
    45→                    if sig.compute:
    46→                        # Pass filepath to compute fns that accept it (tree-sitter signals).
    47→                        accepts_filepath = "_filepath" in inspect.signature(
    48→                            sig.compute
    49→                        ).parameters
    50→                        if accepts_filepath:
    51→                            result = sig.compute(content, lines, _filepath=filepath)
    52→                        else:
    53→                            result = sig.compute(content, lines)
    54→                        if result:
    55→                            count, label = result
    56→                            file_signals.append(label)
    57→                            excess = (
    58→                                max(0, count - sig.threshold) if sig.threshold else count
    59→                            )
    60→                            score += excess * sig.weight
    61→                    elif sig.pattern:
    62→                        count = len(re.findall(sig.pattern, content, re.MULTILINE))
    63→                        if count > sig.threshold:
    64→                            file_signals.append(f"{count} {sig.name}")
    65→                            score += (count - sig.threshold) * sig.weight
    66→                except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError, AttributeError, re.error) as exc:
    67→                    log_best_effort_failure(
    68→                        logger,
    69→                        f"compute complexity signal '{sig.name}' for {filepath}",
    70→                        exc,
    71→                    )
    72→                    continue
    73→
    74→            if file_signals and score >= threshold:
    75→                entries.append(
    76→                    {
    77→                        "file": filepath,
    78→                        "loc": loc,
    79→                        "score": score,
    80→                        "signals": file_signals,
```

```
    50→def _signature_group_keys(name: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    51→    """Return grouping keys: exact name and selected naming-pattern buckets."""
    52→    keys = [("name", name)]
    53→    normalized = name.lstrip("_")
    54→    if normalized.startswith("phase_") and len(normalized) > len("phase_"):
    55→        keys.append(("pattern", "phase_*"))
    56→    return keys
    57→
    58→
    59→def detect_signature_variance(
    60→    functions: list[FunctionInfo],
    61→    min_occurrences: int = 3,
    62→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    63→    """Find function names appearing 3+ times across files with different signatures.
    64→
    65→    Returns (entries, total_functions_checked).
    66→
    67→    Each entry represents a function name group where signatures differ:
    68→    {
    69→        "name": str,           # function name
    70→        "occurrences": int,    # how many definitions
    71→        "files": list[str],    # distinct files
    72→        "variants": list[dict] # [{file, line, params, param_count}]
    73→    }
    74→    """
    75→    # Group by exact function name and select naming-pattern groups.
    76→    by_group: dict[tuple[str, str], list[FunctionInfo]] = defaultdict(list)
    77→    for fn in functions:
    78→        normalized_name = fn.name.lstrip("_")
    79→        is_phase_pattern = normalized_name.startswith("phase_")
    80→        if fn.name.startswith("_") and not fn.name.startswith("__") and not is_phase_pattern:
```

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
    10→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    11→    "path_scoped_findings",
    12→    "is_ignored",
    13→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    14→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    15→    "add_ignore",
    16→    "make_finding",
    17→]
    18→
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    20→    Finding,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    ensure_state_defaults,
    23→    utc_now,
    24→    validate_state_invariants,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    27→
    28→
    29→def path_scoped_findings(
    30→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    34→    return {
    35→        finding_id: finding
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    37→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    55→    scan_path: str | None,
    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:
    59→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    60→    in_scope = 0
    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    65→            continue
    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    79→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    84→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        raw_base = pattern.rstrip("/")
   105→        rel_base = rel(pattern).rstrip("/")
   106→        for base in (raw_base, rel_base):
   107→            if not base:
   108→                continue
   109→            if file == base or file.startswith(base + "/"):
   110→                return pattern
   111→
   112→    return None
   113→
   114→
   115→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   116→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   117→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   118→    matched_ids = [
   119→        finding_id
   120→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   121→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   122→    ]
   123→    now = utc_now()
   124→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   125→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   126→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   127→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   128→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   129→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   130→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   131→
   132→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   133→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   134→    return len(matched_ids)
   135→
   136→
   137→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   138→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   139→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   140→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   141→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   142→    if pattern not in ignores:
   143→        ignores.append(pattern)
   144→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   145→

   149→    file: str,
   150→    name: str,
```

```
147→def make_finding(
   148→    detector: str,
   149→    file: str,
   150→    name: str,
   151→    *,
   152→    tier: int,
   153→    confidence: str,
   154→    summary: str,
   155→    detail: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> Finding:
   157→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   158→    rfile = rel(file)
   159→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   160→    now = utc_now()
   161→    return {
   162→        "id": finding_id,
   163→        "detector": detector,
   164→        "file": rfile,
   165→        "tier": tier,
   166→        "confidence": confidence,
   167→        "summary": summary,
   168→        "detail": detail or {},
   169→        "status": "open",
   170→        "note": None,
   171→        "first_seen": now,
   172→        "last_seen": now,
   173→        "resolved_at": None,
   174→        "reopen_count": 0,
   175→    }
   176→
   177→
   178→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   179→
   180→
   181→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   182→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   183→    if finding_id == pattern:
   184→        return True
   185→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   186→        return True
   187→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   188→        return True
   189→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   190→        return True
   191→    if (
   192→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   193→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   194→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   195→    ):
   196→        return True
   197→
   198→    # Name-segment fallback: bare name matches the last ::segment of the ID
   199→    if "::" not in pattern and "::" in finding_id:
   200→        segments = finding_id.split("::")
   201→        name_segment = segments[-1]
   202→        if name_segment == pattern:
   203→            return True
   204→        # For hashed IDs, also match the descriptive name (second-to-last segment)
   205→        if len(segments) >= 3 and _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment) and segments[-2] == pattern:
   206→            return True
   207→
   208→    return False
   209→
```

```
1→"""Duplicate / near-duplicate function detection via body hashing + difflib similarity.
     2→
     3→Output is clustered: N similar functions produce 1 entry (not N^2/2 pairwise entries).
     4→Each entry contains a representative pair for display plus the full cluster membership.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import difflib
     8→import os
     9→import sys
    10→import time
    11→
    12→
    13→def _build_clusters(
    14→    pairs: list[tuple[int, int, float, str]], n: int
    15→) -> list[list[int]]:
    16→    """Union-find clustering from pairwise matches. Returns list of clusters (size >= 2)."""
    17→    parent = list(range(n))
    18→
    19→    def find(x):
    20→        while parent[x] != x:
    21→            parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]
    22→            x = parent[x]
    23→        return x
    24→
    25→    def union(a, b):
    26→        ra, rb = find(a), find(b)
    27→        if ra != rb:
    28→            parent[ra] = rb
    29→
    30→    for i, j, _, _ in pairs:
    31→        union(i, j)
    32→
    33→    clusters: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
    34→    for i in range(n):
    35→        r = find(i)
    36→        clusters.setdefault(r, []).append(i)
    37→    return [c for c in clusters.values() if len(c) >= 2]
    38→
    39→
    40→def _dupes_debug_settings() -> tuple[bool, int]:
    41→    """Read dupes debug flags from environment."""
    42→    debug = os.getenv("DESLOPPIFY_DUPES_DEBUG", "").lower() in {
    43→        "1",
    44→        "true",
    45→        "yes",
    46→        "on",
    47→    }
    48→    try:
    49→        debug_every = max(
    50→            1, int(os.getenv("DESLOPPIFY_DUPES_DEBUG_EVERY", "100") or "100")
    51→        )
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        debug_every = 100
    54→    return debug, debug_every
    55→
    56→
    57→def _pair_key(fn_a, fn_b) -> tuple[str, str]:
    58→    """Build a stable pair key for duplicate tracking."""
    59→    def _identity(fn) -> str:
    60→        end_line = getattr(fn, "end_line", None)
    61→        if not isinstance(end_line, int):
    62→            end_line = int(getattr(fn, "line", 0)) + int(getattr(fn, "loc", 0))
    63→        return f"{fn.file}:{fn.name}:{fn.line}:{end_line}"
    64→
    65→    return (_identity(fn_a), _identity(fn_b))
    66→
    67→
    68→def _collect_exact_duplicate_pairs(
    69→    functions, seen_pairs: set[tuple[str, str]]
    70→) -> list[tuple[int, int, float, str]]:
    71→    """Collect exact duplicate pairs (same normalized body hash)."""
    72→    by_hash: dict[str, list[int]] = {}
    73→    for idx, fn in enumerate(functions):
    74→        by_hash.setdefault(fn.body_hash, []).append(idx)
    75→
    76→    exact_pairs: list[tuple[int, int, float, str]] = []
    77→    for idxs in by_hash.values():
    78→        if len(idxs) < 2:
    79→            continue
    80→        for i_pos in range(len(idxs)):
```

```
1→"""Structural signal helpers shared across language phase implementations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.text_api import PROJECT_ROOT
     8→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import resolve_path
     9→from desloppify.state import Finding, make_finding
    10→
    11→
    12→def add_structural_signal(structural: dict, file: str, signal: str, detail: dict):
    13→    """Add a complexity signal to the per-file structural dict.
    14→
    15→    Accumulates signals per file so they can be merged into tiered findings.
    16→    """
    17→    f = resolve_path(file)
    18→    structural.setdefault(f, {"signals": [], "detail": {}})
    19→    structural[f]["signals"].append(signal)
    20→    structural[f]["detail"].update(detail)
    21→
    22→
    23→def merge_structural_signals(
    24→    structural: dict,
    25→    stderr_fn,
    26→    *,
    27→    complexity_only_min: int = 35,
    28→) -> list[Finding]:
    29→    """Convert per-file structural signals into tiered findings.
    30→
    31→    3+ signals -> T4/high (needs decomposition).
    32→    1-2 signals -> T3/medium.
    33→    Complexity-only files (no large/god signals) need score >= complexity_only_min
    34→    to be flagged — lower complexity in small files is normal, not decomposition-worthy.
    35→    """
    36→    results = []
    37→    suppressed = 0
    38→    for filepath, data in structural.items():
    39→        if "loc" not in data["detail"]:
    40→            try:
    41→                p = (
    42→                    Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
    43→                )
    44→                data["detail"]["loc"] = len(p.read_text().splitlines())
    45→            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
    46→                data["detail"]["loc"] = 0
    47→
    48→        # Suppress complexity-only findings below the elevated threshold.
    49→        signals = data["signals"]
    50→        is_complexity_only = all(s.startswith("complexity") for s in signals)
    51→        if is_complexity_only:
    52→            score = data["detail"].get("complexity_score", 0)
    53→            if score < complexity_only_min:
    54→                suppressed += 1
    55→                continue
    56→
    57→        signal_count = len(signals)
    58→        tier = 4 if signal_count >= 3 else 3
    59→        confidence = "high" if signal_count >= 3 else "medium"
    60→        summary = "Needs decomposition: " + " / ".join(signals)
    61→        results.append(
    62→            make_finding(
    63→                "structural",
    64→                filepath,
    65→                "",
    66→                tier=tier,
    67→                confidence=confidence,
    68→                summary=summary,
    69→                detail=data["detail"],
    70→            )
    71→        )
    72→    if suppressed:
    73→        stderr_fn(
    74→            "         "
    75→            f"{suppressed} complexity-only files below threshold (< {complexity_only_min})"
    76→        )
    77→    stderr_fn(f"         -> {len(results)} structural findings")
    78→    return results
    79→
    80→
    81→__all__ = ["add_structural_signal", "merge_structural_signals"]
    82→
```

```
   110→def _scan_root_from_files(files: list[str]) -> Path | None:
   111→    """Derive the common ancestor directory from a list of file paths."""
   112→    import os
   113→
   114→    py_files = [f for f in files if f.endswith(".py")]
   115→    if not py_files:
   116→        return None
   117→    try:
   118→        common = Path(os.path.commonpath(py_files))
   119→        return common if common.is_dir() else common.parent
   120→    except ValueError:
   121→        return None

   135→            ),
   136→            remediation="Install Bandit: pip install bandit",
   137→            tool="bandit",

   139→        )
   140→
   141→    def scan_coverage_prerequisites(self) -> list[DetectorCoverageStatus]:
   142→        if shutil.which("bandit") is not None:
   143→            return []
   144→        return [self._missing_bandit_coverage()]
   145→
   146→    def detect_lang_security_detailed(self, files, zone_map) -> LangSecurityResult:
   147→        scan_root = _scan_root_from_files(files)
   148→        if scan_root is None:
```

```
1→"""Mixed concerns detection (UI + data fetching + transforms in one file)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import re
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.text_api import PROJECT_ROOT
    12→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    13→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import find_tsx_files, rel
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→def detect_mixed_concerns(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
    20→    """Find files that mix UI rendering with data fetching, state management, and business logic.
    21→
    22→    Heuristic: a .tsx file that has both JSX returns AND direct API/supabase calls
    23→    or both UI components AND heavy data transformation.
    24→
    25→    Returns (entries, total_files_checked).
    26→    """
    27→    files = find_tsx_files(path)
    28→    entries = []
    29→    for filepath in files:
    30→        try:
    31→            p = (
    32→                Path(filepath)
    33→                if Path(filepath).is_absolute()
    34→                else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
    35→            )
    36→            content = p.read_text()
    37→            loc = len(content.splitlines())
    38→            if loc < 100:
    39→                continue
    40→
    41→            concerns = []
    42→
    43→            # UI rendering
    44→            has_jsx = bool(re.search(r"return\s*\(?\s*<", content))
    45→            if has_jsx:
    46→                concerns.append("jsx_rendering")
    47→
    48→            # Data fetching
    49→            has_fetch = bool(
    50→                re.search(r"useQuery|useMutation|supabase\.|fetch\(|axios", content)
    51→            )
    52→            if has_fetch:
    53→                concerns.append("data_fetching")
    54→
    55→            # Direct supabase calls (should be in hooks/services)
    56→            has_supabase = bool(re.search(r"supabase\.\w+\.\w+\.\w+", content))
    57→            if has_supabase:
    58→                concerns.append("direct_supabase")
    59→
    60→            # Heavy data transformation
    61→            transform_patterns = len(
    62→                re.findall(r"\.(map|filter|reduce|sort|flatMap)\s*\(", content)
    63→            )
    64→            if transform_patterns >= 3:
    65→                concerns.append(f"data_transforms({transform_patterns})")
    66→
    67→            # Event handler definitions (>5 = probably doing too much)
    68→            handler_count = len(re.findall(r"(?:const|function)\s+handle\w+", content))
    69→            if handler_count >= 5:
    70→                concerns.append(f"handlers({handler_count})")
    71→
    72→            # Flag if 3+ concern types in one file
    73→            if len(concerns) >= 3:
    74→                entries.append(
    75→                    {
    76→                        "file": filepath,
    77→                        "loc": loc,
    78→                        "concerns": concerns,
    79→                        "concern_count": len(concerns),
    80→                    }
```

```
1→"""Responsibility cohesion detector for dumping-ground modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import ast
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.text_api import PROJECT_ROOT
     9→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import find_py_files
    10→
    11→
    12→def _extract_top_level_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> list[ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef]:
    13→    return [
    14→        node
    15→        for node in tree.body
    16→        if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef)
    17→    ]
    18→
    19→
    20→def _collect_import_alias_roots(tree: ast.Module) -> dict[str, str]:
    21→    alias_roots: dict[str, str] = {}
    22→    for node in tree.body:
    23→        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
    24→            for alias in node.names:
    25→                local = alias.asname or alias.name.split(".")[0]
    26→                alias_roots[local] = alias.name.split(".")[0]
    27→        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
    28→            module = (node.module or "").split(".")[0]
    29→            for alias in node.names:
    30→                local = alias.asname or alias.name
    31→                alias_roots[local] = module or alias.name.split(".")[0]
    32→    return alias_roots
    33→
    34→
    35→def _function_calls(fn: ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef, function_names: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    36→    calls: set[str] = set()
    37→    for node in ast.walk(fn):
    38→        if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name):
    39→            if node.func.id in function_names and node.func.id != fn.name:
    40→                calls.add(node.func.id)
    41→    return calls
    42→
    43→
    44→def _function_import_roots(
    45→    fn: ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef, alias_roots: dict[str, str]
    46→) -> set[str]:
    47→    roots: set[str] = set()
    48→    for node in ast.walk(fn):
    49→        if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
    50→            root = alias_roots.get(node.id)
    51→            if root:
    52→                roots.add(root)
    53→    return roots
    54→
    55→
    56→def _connected_components(function_names: list[str], adjacency: dict[str, set[str]]) -> list[list[str]]:
    57→    unvisited = set(function_names)
    58→    comps: list[list[str]] = []
    59→    while unvisited:
    60→        seed = next(iter(unvisited))
    61→        stack = [seed]
    62→        comp: list[str] = []
    63→        unvisited.remove(seed)
    64→        while stack:
    65→            name = stack.pop()
    66→            comp.append(name)
    67→            for neighbor in adjacency.get(name, set()):
    68→                if neighbor not in unvisited:
    69→                    continue
    70→                unvisited.remove(neighbor)
    71→                stack.append(neighbor)
    72→        comps.append(sorted(comp))
    73→    comps.sort(key=lambda c: (-len(c), c[0]))
    74→    return comps
    75→
    76→
    77→def _name_family(name: str) -> str:
    78→    return name.split("_", 1)[0] if "_" in name else name
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_responsibility_cohesion(
    82→    path: Path,
    83→    *,
    84→    min_loc: int = 200,
    85→    min_functions: int = 8,
    86→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    87→    """Detect large modules that split into disconnected responsibility clusters."""
    88→    entries: list[dict] = []
    89→    candidates = 0
    90→
    91→    for filepath in find_py_files(path):
    92→        full = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else PROJECT_ROOT / filepath
    93→        try:
    94→            source = full.read_text()
    95→            tree = ast.parse(source, filename=str(full))
    96→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, SyntaxError) as exc:
    97→            # Preserve parse/read context while intentionally skipping broken files.
    98→            _ = (full, exc)
    99→            continue
   100→
```
