Now let me compile my comprehensive audit report. Based on my analysis, I found several key issues:

## COMPREHENSIVE AUDIT REPORT: DUPLICATE & OVERLAPPING DETECTION IN DESLOPPIFY

I've completed a thorough analysis of the desloppify codebase across all detector phases, language implementations, and detector functions. Here are my findings:

### **CRITICAL ISSUE: Missing Import in phases_quality.py**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/phases_quality.py`

**Problem**: Lines 43, 73, 101, 123 reference `state_mod.make_finding()` but the import is missing.

**Current imports** (line 1-20):
```python
from desloppify.state import Finding
from desloppify.core.output_api import log
# Missing: from desloppify import state as state_mod
```

**Impact**: Runtime `NameError` when `phase_mutable_state()`, `phase_layer_violation()`, or `phase_dict_keys()` are called from the Python pipeline.

---

### **CRITICAL ARCHITECTURAL ISSUE #1: Duplicate Structural Detectors Called in Same Phase**

**Pattern**: Three detectors (large/complexity/gods) are called **identically** across multiple languages via shared `run_structural_phase()`.

**Call Sites**:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py:470-507` (shared `run_structural_phase()`)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/phases_runtime.py:50-84` (identical logic in `run_phase_structural()`)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py:224-247` (inline in `phase_structural()`)

**Detectors Invoked**:
1. `detect_large_files()` - flags files exceeding LOC threshold (default 300 for Python, 500 for TS)
2. `detect_complexity()` - flags files with complexity score >= threshold (default 25)
3. `detect_gods()` - flags classes/components matching god-class rules
4. `detect_flat_dirs()` - flags directories with too many files/subdirs

**All merge into single "structural" finding** via `merge_structural_signals()` which consolidates all signals into one finding ID: `structural::{filepath}`. This is CORRECT consolidation — no duplication here.

**Verdict**: **NO ISSUE** — all structural detectors intentionally merge into a single dimension.

---

### **CRITICAL ARCHITECTURAL ISSUE #2: React Sub-Detectors Called Without Phase Separation**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py:581-668` (all in `phase_smells`)

**Problem**: Four independent React pattern detectors are called from a single phase runner but output to a single `"react"` detector ID:
- `detect_state_sync()` (lines 586)
- `detect_context_nesting()` (line 604)  
- `detect_hook_return_bloat()` (line 622)
- `detect_boolean_state_explosion()` (line 643)

**Finding ID Format**: All use `"react"` detector name, differentiated only by the `name` field:
```python
make_finding(
    "react",
    e["file"],
    setter_str,  # or f"nesting::{e['depth']}", "hook_bloat::{e['hook']}", "bool_state::{e['prefix']}"
    ...
)
```

**Issue**: These are four distinct semantic detections (each with different logic, different confidence levels, different remediation):
- State sync: medium confidence, 2-3 related setter calls
- Context nesting: medium confidence, 3+ provider nesting levels
- Hook bloat: medium confidence, 15+ return fields
- Boolean explosion: **LOW** confidence, 5+ boolean useState hooks

**Finding ID would be**: 
```
react::src/components/Form.tsx::setEmail,setName
react::src/components/Form.tsx::nesting::4
react::src/App.tsx::hook_bloat::useFormState
react::src/Dashboard.tsx::bool_state::is
```

**Risk**: Two different issues in the same file would have **different IDs** but same detector name, making them appear as unrelated findings. The confidence levels are **inconsistent** — boolean_state_explosion is flagged as "low" confidence but grouped with "medium" findings.

**Verdict**: **POTENTIAL ISSUE** — These should either be separate phases or use consistent confidence levels.

---

### **CRITICAL ARCHITECTURAL ISSUE #3: Concerns Detector Merged Into Structural Signals**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py:263-270`

**Problem**: `detect_mixed_concerns()` is called in `phase_structural()` and merged via `add_structural_signal()` into the structural findings dict, then merged via `merge_structural_signals()`.

**What it detects**: Files that mix UI rendering + data fetching + transforms (JSX + useQuery/fetch + map/filter, etc.)

**Current behavior**: 
```python
concern_entries, _ = concerns_detector_mod.detect_mixed_concerns(path)
for e in concern_entries:
    add_structural_signal(
        structural,
        e["file"],
        f"mixed: {', '.join(e['concerns'][:3])}",
        {"concerns": e["concerns"]},
    )
results = merge_structural_signals(structural, log)  # Merged as "structural" findings
```

**Issues**:
1. **Semantic mismatch**: Concerns are about responsibility/separation of concerns, NOT about file size/complexity/god-class-ness. It's conceptually misaligned.
2. **Finding ID collision risk**: If a file has BOTH a large-complexity signal AND a concerns signal, it produces ONE `structural::` finding with BOTH signals in the summary. This is fine, but concerns are **semantically different** from structural issues.
3. **Missing in Python**: Concerns detector only exists in TS, not Python. But Python's `responsibility_cohesion` detector serves a similar function (detecting modules with too many unrelated functions). This is a **language-specific semantic overlap**.

**Verdict**: **DESIGN ISSUE** — Concerns detection is conceptually different from structural decomposition and shouldn't be merged with size/complexity/gods signals. Should be a separate detector/dimension.

---

### **ARCHITECTURAL ISSUE #4: Python-Specific Responsibility Cohesion vs. TS Concerns**

**Files**:
- Python: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/python/phases.py:131-170` (`phase_responsibility_cohesion()`)
- TypeScript: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/typescript/phases.py:263-270` (merged into `phase_structural()`)

**What they detect**:
- **Python**: Modules with 8+ top-level functions split into 2+ disconnected call-graph clusters (responsibility cohesion)
- **TypeScript**: Files (100+ LOC) mixing JSX + data fetching + transforms (mixed concerns)

**The overlap**:
- Both detect "too many unrelated things in one module"
- Python is graph-based (function call connectivity), TS is pattern-based (keyword matching)
- Both should have separate findings but they don't in TS

**Verdict**: **MINOR ISSUE** — The detectors are language-appropriate, but TS should have responsibility_cohesion as a **separate phase**, not merged into structural.

---

### **DUPLICATION/OVERLAP ISSUE #5: Dupes vs. Boilerplate Duplication**

**Files**:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/detectors/dupes.py` (function-level dupes via body hash + difflib)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py:81-123` (boilerplate duplication via jscpd)

**Call Sites**:
- `phase_dupes()` → `detect_duplicates()` (lines 76)
- `phase_boilerplate_duplication()` → `detect_with_jscpd()` (line 86)

**Both phases are in the shared_subjective_duplicates_tail**:
```python
shared_subjective_duplicates_tail():
  → detector_phase_subjective_review()
  → (optional pre_duplicates phases)
  → detector_phase_boilerplate_duplication()
  → detector_phase_duplicates()
```

**The distinction**:
- **Dupes**: Exact function body matches + high-similarity function pairs. Output is **function-level** clusters.
- **Boilerplate**: Line/block-level repeated code blocks (type-2 clones: renamed identifiers). Output is **file-level** clusters across files.

**Finding IDs**:
- `dupes::{first_file}::{function_name}` (one finding per cluster)
- `boilerplate_duplication::{first_file}::{cluster_id}` (one finding per boilerplate block cluster)

**NO COLLISION** because different detector names and different scopes (functions vs. code blocks).

**Verdict**: **NO ISSUE** — These are complementary detectors with different scopes.

---

### **OVERLAPPING LOGIC ISSUE #6: Large + Complexity Signals Measure Similar Concepts**

**Location**: `run_structural_phase()` calls both in sequence (lines 471-497 in shared_phases.py)

**The problem**:
- `detect_large_files()`: Flags files with LOC > threshold (simplistic)
- `detect_complexity()`: Flags files with **complexity score** >= threshold

**A file can be flagged by both**:
```
File A: 600 LOC
  → detect_large_files: flags (600 > 500)
  → detect_complexity: flags if (imports_score + nesting_score + ... >= 25)
```

**In merge_structural_signals**:
- Both become signals: ["large (600 LOC)", "complexity score 32"]
- Result: ONE `structural::file_A` finding with **both** signals in detail
- Tier/confidence bumped up if 3+ signals (large + complexity + gods = T4/high)

**Is this a problem?**
- **Intentionally coupled**: Large + high-complexity = higher confidence of decomposition need
- **No finding ID collision**: Same detector, same file
- **Desired behavior**: These reinforce each other

**Verdict**: **NO ISSUE** — This is intentional signal reinforcement.

---

### **MISSING CONSISTENCY ISSUE #7: Signature Variance Called Only Once Correctly**

**Location**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py:418-450`

**Function**: `phase_signature()` calls `detect_signature_variance(functions, min_occurrences=3)` exactly once.

**Detector checks**:
- Function names appearing 3+ times across files
- Excludes allowlisted names (__init__, get, post, etc.)
- Excludes private/underscore functions unless they match phase_* pattern

**No duplicates of this call** detected across codebase.

**Verdict**: **CLEAN** — Previous fix (based on git log) was correct.

def phase_architectural_concerns(path: Path, lang: LangRun) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    entries, potential = concerns_detector_mod.detect_mixed_concerns(path)
    results = []
    for e in entries:
        results.append(make_finding(
            "concerns",  # or "mixed_concerns"
            e["file"],
            f"concerns::{len(e['concerns'])}",
            tier=3,
            confidence="medium",
            summary=f"Mixed concerns: {', '.join(e['concerns'])}",
            detail={"concerns": e["concerns"]},
        ))
    return results, {"concerns": potential}
```

    72→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):

    74→
    75→
    76→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    77→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    78→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    79→        return
    80→    try:
    81→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    82→        _save_config(config)
    83→    except OSError as exc:
    84→        _ = exc
    85→        config["needs_rescan"] = True
    86→
    87→
    88→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    89→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    90→    return bool(
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("overrides")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→        or plan.get("skipped")
    95→    )
    96→
    97→
    98→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    99→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
   100→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
   101→        return False
   102→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
   103→    if recon.resurfaced:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   107→                "cyan",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→    return bool(recon.changes)
   111→
   112→
   113→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   114→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
   115→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   116→    if sync.injected:
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   120→                "cyan",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→    return bool(sync.changes)
   124→
   125→
   126→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   127→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   128→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   129→    if sync.pruned:
   130→        print(
   131→            colorize(
   132→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   133→                "cyan",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if sync.injected:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   140→                "cyan",
   141→            )
   142→        )
   143→    return bool(sync.changes)
   144→
   145→
   146→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   147→    plan: dict[str, object],
   148→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   149→    *,
   150→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   151→    policy=None,
   152→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   153→) -> bool:
   154→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   155→    return bool(auto_cluster_findings(
   156→        plan, state,
   157→        target_strict=target_strict,
   158→        policy=policy,
   159→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   160→    ))
   161→
   162→
   163→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   164→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   165→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   166→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   167→        return False
   168→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   169→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   170→        return False
   171→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   172→    if scores.strict is None:
   173→        return False
   174→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   175→        "strict": scores.strict,
   176→        "overall": scores.overall,
   177→        "objective": scores.objective,
   178→        "verified": scores.verified,
   179→    }
   180→    return True
   181→
   182→
   183→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   184→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   185→) -> bool:
   186→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   187→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   188→        return False
   189→
   190→    try:
   191→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
   192→
   193→        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
   194→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   195→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   196→        return False
   197→    if not queue_empty:
   198→        return False
   199→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   200→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   201→    return True
   202→
   203→
   204→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   205→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   206→    try:
   207→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   208→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   209→        dirty = False
   210→
   211→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   212→            dirty = True
   213→
   214→        unscored_changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   215→        if unscored_changed:
   216→            dirty = True
   217→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system",
   218→                             detail={"changes": True})
   219→
   220→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
   221→        _target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   222→
   223→        # Compute subjective visibility policy once for consistent gating
   224→        from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_subjective_visibility
   225→        _policy = compute_subjective_visibility(runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict)
   226→
   227→        # Detect cycle completion: plan_start_scores is empty when the
   228→        # previous cycle drained the queue and revealed scores.  In that
   229→        # case stale subjective dimensions should be prioritized over new
   230→        # objective findings so the user reviews before a new cycle begins.
   231→        _cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   232→
   233→        stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   234→            plan, runtime.state,
   235→            lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   236→                p, s, policy=_policy, cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
   237→            ),
   238→        )
   239→        if stale_changed:
   240→            dirty = True
   241→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system",
   242→                             detail={"changes": True})
   243→
   244→        auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   245→            plan, runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict, policy=_policy,
   246→            cycle_just_completed=_cycle_just_completed,
   247→        )
   248→        if auto_changed:
   249→            dirty = True
   250→            append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system",
   251→                             detail={"changes": True})
   252→
   253→        triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   254→        if triage_sync.changes:
   255→            dirty = True
   256→            if triage_sync.injected:
   257→                print(
   258→                    colorize(
   259→                        "  Plan: planning mode needed — review findings changed since last triage.",
   260→                        "cyan",
   261→                    )
   262→                )
   263→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system",
   264→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   265→
   266→        checkpoint_sync = sync_score_checkpoint_needed(plan, runtime.state, policy=_policy)
   267→        if checkpoint_sync.changes:
   268→            dirty = True
   269→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_score_checkpoint", actor="system",
   270→                             detail={"injected": True})
   271→
   272→        create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, runtime.state, policy=_policy)
   273→        if create_plan_sync.changes:
   274→            dirty = True
   275→            if create_plan_sync.injected:
   276→                print(
   277→                    colorize(
   278→                        "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   279→                        "cyan",
   280→                    )
   281→                )
   282→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system",
   283→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   284→
   285→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   286→        if seeded:
   287→            dirty = True
   288→            append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system",
   289→                             detail={})
   290→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   291→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   292→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   293→            dirty = True
   294→            append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system",
   295→                             detail={})
   296→
   297→        if dirty:
   298→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   299→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   300→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)
   301→
   302→
   303→def _state_subjective_assessments(
   304→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   305→) -> dict[str, object]:
   306→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
   307→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   308→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   309→        return assessments
   310→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   311→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   312→    )
   313→
   314→
   315→def _state_lang_capabilities(
   316→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   317→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   318→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
   319→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   320→    if capabilities is None:
   321→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   322→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   323→        return normalized
   324→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   325→        return capabilities
   326→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   327→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   328→    )
   329→
   330→
   331→def _state_findings(state: state_mod.StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   332→    """Return normalized finding map from state."""
   333→    findings = state.get("findings")
   334→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
   335→        return findings
   336→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   337→        "state.findings must be an object; state file appears corrupted"
   338→    )
   339→
   340→
   341→def _subjective_reset_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   342→    """Resolve subjective dimensions that should reset on scan baseline reset."""
   343→    return resettable_default_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name)
   344→
   345→
   346→@dataclass
   347→class ScanRuntime:
   348→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   349→
   350→    args: argparse.Namespace
   351→    state_path: Path | None
   352→    state: state_mod.StateModel
   353→    path: Path
   354→    config: dict[str, object]
   355→    lang: LangRun | None
   356→    lang_label: str
   357→    profile: str
   358→    effective_include_slow: bool
   359→    zone_overrides: dict[str, object] | None
   360→    reset_subjective_count: int = 0
   361→    expired_manual_override_count: int = 0
   362→    coverage_warnings: list[DetectorCoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
   363→
   364→
   365→@dataclass
   366→class ScanMergeResult:
   367→    """State merge outputs and previous score snapshots."""
   368→
   369→    diff: dict[str, object]
   370→    prev_overall: float | None
   371→    prev_objective: float | None
   372→    prev_strict: float | None
   373→    prev_verified: float | None
   374→    prev_dim_scores: dict[str, object]
   375→
   376→
   377→@dataclass
   378→class ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   379→    """Noise budget settings and hidden finding counts for this scan."""
   380→
   381→    noise_budget: int
   382→    global_noise_budget: int
   383→    budget_warning: str | None
   384→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
   385→    hidden_total: int
   386→
   387→
   388→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   389→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   390→    config: dict[str, object],
   391→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   392→    lang: LangRun | None,
   393→) -> LangRun | None:
   394→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   395→    if not lang:
   396→        return None
   397→
   398→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   399→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   400→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   401→        lang,
   402→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   403→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   404→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   405→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   406→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   407→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   408→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   409→        ),
   410→    )
   411→
   412→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   413→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   414→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   415→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   416→    }
   417→    return runtime_lang
   418→
   419→
   420→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   421→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   422→    *,
   423→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   424→) -> int:
   425→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   426→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   427→
   428→    reset_keys = {
   429→        key.strip()
   430→        for key in assessments
   431→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   432→    }
   433→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   434→
   435→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   436→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   437→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   438→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   439→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   440→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   441→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   442→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   443→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   444→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   445→            payload.pop("components", None)
   446→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   447→            continue
   448→        assessments[key] = {
   449→            "score": 0.0,
   450→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   451→            "assessed_at": now,
   452→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   453→            "placeholder": True,
   454→        }
   455→    return len(reset_keys)
   456→
   457→
   458→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   459→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   460→) -> int:
   461→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   462→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   463→
   464→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   465→    expired = 0
   466→    for payload in assessments.values():
   467→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   468→            continue
   469→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   470→            continue
   471→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   472→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   473→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   474→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   475→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   476→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   477→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   478→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   479→        payload.pop("components", None)
   480→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   481→        expired += 1
   482→    return expired
   483→
   484→
   485→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   486→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   487→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   488→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   489→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   490→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   491→    path = Path(args.path)
   492→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   493→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   494→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   495→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   496→        state
   497→    )
   498→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   499→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   500→            state,
   501→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   502→        )
   503→
   504→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   505→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   506→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   507→
   508→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   509→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   510→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   511→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   512→
   513→    return ScanRuntime(
   514→        args=args,
   515→        state_path=state_file,
   516→        state=state,
   517→        path=path,
   518→        config=config,
   519→        lang=lang,
   520→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   521→        profile=profile,
   522→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   523→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   524→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   525→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   526→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   527→    )
   528→
   529→
   530→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   531→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   532→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   533→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   534→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   535→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   536→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   537→        return findings
   538→
   539→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   540→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   541→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   542→    )
   543→    if not stale:
   544→        return findings
   545→
   546→    augmented = list(findings)
   547→    augmented.extend(stale)
   548→    for stale_finding in stale:
   549→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   550→    return augmented
   551→
   552→
   553→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   554→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   555→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   556→    *,
   557→    decay_scans: int,
   558→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   559→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   560→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   561→        findings,
   562→        state=runtime.state,
   563→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   564→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   565→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   566→    )
   567→
   568→
   569→def run_scan_generation(
   570→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   571→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   572→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   573→    enable_file_cache()
   574→    enable_parse_cache()
   575→    try:
   576→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   577→            runtime.path,
   578→            lang=runtime.lang,
   579→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   580→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   581→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   582→                profile=runtime.profile,
   583→            ),
   584→        )
   585→    finally:
   586→        disable_parse_cache()
   587→        disable_file_cache()
   588→
   589→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   590→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   591→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   592→    )
   593→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   594→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   595→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   596→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   597→    )
   598→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   599→        findings,
   600→        runtime,
   601→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   602→    )
   603→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   604→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   605→
   606→
   607→def merge_scan_results(
   608→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   609→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   610→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   611→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   612→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   613→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   614→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   615→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   616→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   617→
   618→    if not path_changed:
   619→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   620→    else:
   621→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   622→    prev_dim_scores = (
   623→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   624→    )
   625→
   626→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   627→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   628→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   629→
   630→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   631→
   632→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   633→        runtime.state,
   634→        findings,
   635→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   636→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   637→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   638→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   639→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   640→            potentials=potentials,
   641→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   642→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   643→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   644→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   645→        ),
   646→    )
   647→
   648→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   649→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   650→    )
   651→    state_mod.save_state(
   652→        runtime.state,
   653→        runtime.state_path,
   654→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   655→    )
   656→
   657→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   658→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   659→
   660→    return ScanMergeResult(
   661→        diff=diff,
   662→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   663→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   664→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   665→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   666→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   667→    )
   668→
   669→
   670→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   671→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   672→    config: dict[str, object],
   673→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   674→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   675→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   676→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   677→    )
   678→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   679→    open_findings = [
   680→        finding
   681→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   682→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   683→        ).values()
   684→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   685→    ]
   686→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   687→        open_findings,
   688→        budget=noise_budget,
   689→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   690→    )
   691→
   692→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   693→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   694→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   695→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   696→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   697→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   698→    )
   699→
   700→
   701→def persist_reminder_history(
   702→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   703→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   704→) -> None:
   705→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   706→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   707→        return
   708→
   709→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   710→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   711→    state_mod.save_state(
   712→        runtime.state,
   713→        runtime.state_path,
   714→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   715→    )
   716→
   717→
   718→__all__ = [
   719→    "ScanStateContractError",
   720→    "ScanMergeResult",
   721→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   722→    "ScanRuntime",
   723→    "merge_scan_results",
   724→    "persist_reminder_history",
   725→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   726→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   727→    "run_scan_generation",
   728→]
   729→

    56→
    57→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    58→    """Print the scan header line."""
    59→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    60→
    61→
    62→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    63→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    64→    lines = [
    65→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    66→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    67→    ]
    68→    print("\n".join(lines))
    69→
    70→
    71→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    72→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
   101→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   102→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   103→        if impact:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   105→        if remediation:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   110→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.plan_nudge import print_plan_workflow_nudge
   111→
   112→    print_plan_workflow_nudge(state)
   113→
   114→
   115→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   116→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   117→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   118→    try:
   119→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   120→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   121→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   122→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   123→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   124→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   125→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   126→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   127→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   128→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   129→        runtime,
   130→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   131→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   132→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   133→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   134→    )
   135→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   136→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Subjective reset "
   140→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   141→                "yellow",
   142→            )
   143→        )
   144→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   145→        print(
   146→            colorize(
   147→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   148→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   149→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   150→                "yellow",
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   154→
   155→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   156→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   157→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   158→
   159→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   160→
   161→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   162→
   163→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   164→    show_score_delta(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        merge.prev_overall,
   167→        merge.prev_objective,
   168→        merge.prev_strict,
   169→        merge.prev_verified,
   170→        target_strict=target_value,
   171→    )
   172→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   173→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   174→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   175→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   176→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   177→
   178→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   179→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   180→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   181→
   182→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   183→        merge.diff,
   184→        runtime.state,
   185→        runtime.lang,
   186→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   187→    )
   188→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   189→
   190→    write_query(
   191→        build_scan_query_payload(
   192→            runtime.state,
   193→            runtime.config,
   194→            runtime.profile,
   195→            merge.diff,
   196→            warnings,
   197→            narrative,
   198→            merge,
   199→            noise,
   200→        ),
   201→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   205→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   206→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   207→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   208→        badge_path = badge_emit
   209→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   210→    auto_update_skill()
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "cmd_scan",
   215→]
   216→

1→"""Finding generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.text_api import PROJECT_ROOT
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.core.file_paths import rel
    13→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    16→from desloppify.state import Finding
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, PROJECT_ROOT)
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   151→    )
   152→
```

    51→class Finding(TypedDict):
    52→    """The central data structure: a normalized finding from any detector."""

    55→    detector: str

   192→def utc_now() -> str:
   193→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   194→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   195→
   196→
   197→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   198→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   199→    return {
   200→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   201→        "created": utc_now(),
   202→        "last_scan": None,
   203→        "scan_count": 0,
   204→        "overall_score": 0,
   205→        "objective_score": 0,
   206→        "strict_score": 0,
   207→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   208→        "stats": {},
   209→        "findings": {},
   210→        "scan_coverage": {},
   211→        "score_confidence": {},
   212→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   213→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   214→    }
   215→
   216→
   217→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   218→    try:
   219→        parsed = int(value)
   220→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   221→        return default
   222→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   223→
   224→
   225→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   226→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   227→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   228→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   229→
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   231→        state["findings"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   233→        state["stats"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   235→        state["scan_history"] = []
   236→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   237→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   238→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   239→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   240→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   241→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   242→
   243→    findings = state["findings"]
   244→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   245→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   246→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   247→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   248→            continue
   249→
   250→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)

1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
    10→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    11→    "path_scoped_findings",
    12→    "is_ignored",
    13→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    14→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    15→    "add_ignore",
    16→    "make_finding",
    17→]
    18→
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    20→    Finding,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    ensure_state_defaults,
    23→    utc_now,
    24→    validate_state_invariants,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    27→
    28→
    29→def path_scoped_findings(
    30→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    34→    return {
    35→        finding_id: finding
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    37→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    55→    scan_path: str | None,
    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:
    59→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    60→    in_scope = 0
    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    65→            continue
    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    79→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    84→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        raw_base = pattern.rstrip("/")
   105→        rel_base = rel(pattern).rstrip("/")
   106→        for base in (raw_base, rel_base):
   107→            if not base:
   108→                continue
   109→            if file == base or file.startswith(base + "/"):
   110→                return pattern
   111→
   112→    return None
   113→
   114→
   115→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   116→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   117→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   118→    matched_ids = [
   119→        finding_id
   120→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   121→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   122→    ]
   123→    now = utc_now()
   124→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   125→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   126→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   127→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   128→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   129→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   130→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   131→
   132→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   133→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   134→    return len(matched_ids)
   135→
   136→
   137→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   138→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   139→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   140→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   141→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   142→    if pattern not in ignores:
   143→        ignores.append(pattern)
   144→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   145→
   146→

   148→    detector: str,
   149→    file: str,
   150→    name: str,

147→def make_finding(
   148→    detector: str,
   149→    file: str,
   150→    name: str,
   151→    *,
   152→    tier: int,
   153→    confidence: str,
   154→    summary: str,
   155→    detail: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> Finding:
   157→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   158→    rfile = rel(file)
   159→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   160→    now = utc_now()
   161→    return {
   162→        "id": finding_id,
   163→        "detector": detector,
   164→        "file": rfile,
   165→        "tier": tier,
   166→        "confidence": confidence,
   167→        "summary": summary,
   168→        "detail": detail or {},
   169→        "status": "open",
   170→        "note": None,
   171→        "first_seen": now,
   172→        "last_seen": now,
   173→        "resolved_at": None,
   174→        "reopen_count": 0,
   175→    }
   176→
   177→
   178→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   179→
   180→
   181→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   182→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   183→    if finding_id == pattern:
   184→        return True
   185→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   186→        return True
   187→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   188→        return True
   189→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   190→        return True
   191→    if (
   192→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   193→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   194→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   195→    ):
   196→        return True
   197→
   198→    # Name-segment fallback: bare name matches the last ::segment of the ID
   199→    if "::" not in pattern and "::" in finding_id:
   200→        segments = finding_id.split("::")
   201→        name_segment = segments[-1]
   202→        if name_segment == pattern:
   203→            return True
   204→        # For hashed IDs, also match the descriptive name (second-to-last segment)
   205→        if len(segments) >= 3 and _HEX8_RE.match(name_segment) and segments[-2] == pattern:
   206→            return True

1→"""Scan merge/update operations for persisted findings state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "MergeScanOptions",
    10→    "merge_scan",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_findings import (
    14→    auto_resolve_disappeared,
    15→    find_suspect_detectors,
    16→    upsert_findings,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_history import (
    19→    _append_scan_history,
    20→    _build_merge_diff,
    21→    _compute_suppression,
    22→    _merge_scan_inputs,
    23→    _record_scan_metadata,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    26→    ScanDiff,
    27→    StateModel,
    28→    ensure_state_defaults,
    29→    utc_now,
    30→    validate_state_invariants,
    31→)
    32→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,
   209→        suspect_detectors=suspect_detectors,
   210→        chronic_reopeners=chronic_reopeners,
   211→        skipped_other_lang=skipped_other_lang,
   212→        skipped_out_of_scope=skipped_out_of_scope,
   213→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   214→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   215→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   216→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   217→    )
   218→
```

1→"""Finding upsert/auto-resolve helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     6→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import matches_exclusion
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open findings but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for finding in existing.values():
    21→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' findings enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always marked suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them.
    30→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    31→    suspect: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    34→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    35→            suspect.add(detector)
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def auto_resolve_disappeared(
    50→    existing: dict,
    51→    current_ids: set[str],
    52→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    53→    now: str,
    54→    *,
    55→    lang: str | None,
    56→    scan_path: str | None,
    57→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    58→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    59→    """Auto-resolve open/wontfix/fixed/false_positive findings absent from scan.
    60→
    61→    Returns (resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors).
    62→    """
    63→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
    64→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    65→
    66→    for finding_id, previous in existing.items():
    67→        if finding_id in current_ids or previous["status"] not in (
    68→            "open",
    69→            "wontfix",
    70→            "fixed",
    71→            "false_positive",
    72→        ):
    73→            continue
    74→
    75→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
    76→            skipped_other_lang += 1
    77→            continue
    78→
    79→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
    80→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    81→            if (
    82→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
    83→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
    84→            ):
    85→                skipped_out_of_scope += 1
    86→                continue
    87→
    88→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
    92→            continue
    93→
    94→        previous_status = previous["status"]
    95→        previous["status"] = "auto_resolved"
    96→        previous["resolved_at"] = now
    97→        previous["suppressed"] = False
    98→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
    99→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   100→        previous["resolution_attestation"] = {
   101→            "kind": "scan_verified",
   102→            "text": "Disappeared from detector output",
   103→            "attested_at": now,
   104→            "scan_verified": True,
   105→        }
   106→        previous["note"] = (
   107→            "Fixed despite wontfix — disappeared from scan (was wontfix)"
   108→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   109→            else "Disappeared from scan — likely fixed"
   110→        )
   111→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   112→        resolved += 1
   113→
   114→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   115→
   116→
   117→def upsert_findings(
   118→    existing: dict,
   119→    current_findings: list[dict],
   120→    ignore: list[str],
   121→    now: str,
   122→    *,
   123→    lang: str | None,
   124→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   125→    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
   126→
   127→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   128→    """
   129→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   130→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   131→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   132→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   133→
   134→    for finding in current_findings:
   135→        finding_id = finding["id"]
   136→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   137→        current_ids.add(finding_id)
   138→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   139→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
   140→        if matched_ignore:
   141→            ignored_count += 1
   142→
   143→        if lang:
   144→            finding["lang"] = lang
   145→
   146→        if finding_id not in existing:
   147→            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
   148→            if matched_ignore:
   149→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
   150→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   151→                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   152→                continue
   153→            new_count += 1
   154→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        previous = existing[finding_id]
   158→        previous.update(
   159→            last_seen=now,
   160→            tier=finding["tier"],
   161→            confidence=finding["confidence"],
   162→            summary=finding["summary"],
   163→            detail=finding.get("detail", {}),
   164→        )
   165→        if "zone" in finding:
   166→            previous["zone"] = finding["zone"]
   167→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   168→            previous["lang"] = lang
   169→
   170→        if matched_ignore:
   171→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   172→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   173→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   174→            continue
   175→
   176→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   177→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   178→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   179→
   180→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
   181→            # subjective_review findings are condition-based.  When just
   182→            # auto-resolved by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   183→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   184→            if (
   185→                detector == "subjective_review"
   186→                and previous["status"] == "auto_resolved"
   187→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   188→            ):
   189→                continue
   190→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   191→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   192→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   193→            previous.update(
   194→                status="open",
   195→                resolved_at=None,
   196→                note=(
   197→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   198→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   199→                ),
   200→            )
   201→            reopened_count += 1
   202→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   203→
   204→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = [
   208→    "auto_resolve_disappeared",
   209→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   210→    "upsert_findings",
   211→]
   212→
```
